    <script type="module">
        import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-app.js";
        import { getAuth, signInWithEmailAndPassword, createUserWithEmailAndPassword, signOut, onAuthStateChanged } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";
        import { getDatabase, ref, set, get, remove, onValue } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-database.js";

        const firebaseConfig = {
            apiKey: "AIzaSyAc3pD7JS_Q0MmlLaRBEvMzs7JAqhp7ws0",
            authDomain: "vishal-32e2d.firebaseapp.com",
            projectId: "vishal-32e2d",
            storageBucket: "vishal-32e2d.firebasestorage.app",
            messagingSenderId: "532266803383",
            appId: "1:532266803383:web:26128e15c308b0affc6d32"
        };

        let app, auth, database;
        try {
            app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
            auth = getAuth(app);
            database = getDatabase(app);
            console.log('Firebase initialized successfully');
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Firebase initialization error:', error);
            document.getElementById('authModal').style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.display = 'block';
        }

        if (auth) {
            onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
                const authModal = document.getElementById('authModal');
                if (user || localStorage.getItem('guest_login') === 'true') {
                    if (authModal) authModal.style.display = 'none';
                    document.body.style.display = 'block';
                } else {
                    if (authModal) authModal.style.display = 'flex';
                    document.body.style.display = 'block';
                }
            });
        }

        window.signIn = function () {
            if (!auth || !database) {
                alert('Authentication not available');
                return;
            }
            const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;

            if (email === 'admin@admin.com' && password === 'admin123') {
                document.getElementById('authModal').style.display = 'none';
                document.body.style.display = 'block';
                localStorage.setItem('guest_login', 'true');
                return;
            }

            const emailKey = email.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_');
            const sessionRef = ref(database, `sessions/${emailKey}`);
            const sessionId = Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);

            get(sessionRef).then((snapshot) => {
                set(sessionRef, {
                    sessionId: sessionId,
                    timestamp: Date.now(),
                    userAgent: navigator.userAgent,
                    loginTime: new Date().toISOString()
                });

                signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password)
                    .then((userCredential) => {
                        window.currentSessionId = sessionId;
                        window.currentSessionRef = sessionRef;
                        window.sessionCheckInterval = setInterval(() => {
                            get(sessionRef).then((checkSnapshot) => {
                                if (checkSnapshot.exists()) {
                                    const currentData = checkSnapshot.val();
                                    if (currentData.sessionId !== window.currentSessionId) {
                                        alert('Your session has been terminated because this account was logged in from another device.');
                                        signOut(auth);
                                    }
                                }
                            });
                        }, 5000);
                        console.log('Signed in:', userCredential.user, 'Session ID:', sessionId);
                    })
                    .catch((error) => {
                        console.error('Sign in error:', error);
                        alert('Login failed: ' + error.message);
                    });
            }).catch((error) => {
                console.error('Session check error:', error);
                alert('Login failed: ' + error.message);
            });
        };

        window.signUp = function () {
            if (!auth) {
                alert('Authentication not available');
                return;
            }
            const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
            if (!email || !password) {
                alert('Please enter email and password');
                return;
            }
            createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password)
                .then((userCredential) => {
                    alert('Account created successfully! You can now log in.');
                })
                .catch((error) => {
                    console.error('Sign up error:', error);
                    alert('Signup failed: ' + error.message);
                });
        };

        window.signOut = function () {
            if (!auth) return;
            if (window.sessionCheckInterval) {
                clearInterval(window.sessionCheckInterval);
            }
            if (window.currentSessionRef && database) {
                remove(window.currentSessionRef).catch((error) => {
                    console.error('Failed to clear session:', error);
                });
            }
            const keys = Object.keys(localStorage);
            keys.forEach(key => {
                if (key.startsWith('login_')) {
                    localStorage.removeItem(key);
                }
            });
            localStorage.removeItem('guest_login');
            signOut(auth).then(() => {
                console.log('Signed out');
            }).catch((error) => {
                console.error('Sign out error:', error);
            });
        };
    </script>

</head>

        <div class="card" id="seedsSection" style="display:none; margin-top:12px;">

            <div class="section-title">🌱 Seeds and fruits</div>

            <div class="grid">

                        <button class="copy-btn" id="sellFruitsVeggiesBtn"
                            style="flex: 1; padding: 8px 12px; background: #3b82f6; color: white; border: none; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                            Selling fruits and vegetables
                        </button>
                        <button class="copy-btn" id="buyFruitsVeggiesBtn"
                            style="flex: 1; padding: 8px 12px; background: #10b981; color: white; border: none; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                            Buying fruits and vegetables
                        </button>
                    </div>

                </div>

                <div class="field sm-6" id="seedsContainer" style="display:none;">

                    <label>Seeds (click to select)</label>

            <div class="field sm-3" id="seedsQuantityField">

            <div class="actions" style="margin-top:12px; align-items:center;">

                <button class="primary" id="seedsCopyBtn">Copy</button>

        <script>
            // Seeds and fruits section handling
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
                const seedsCategory = document.getElementById('seedsCategory');
                const fruitsContainer = document.getElementById('fruitsContainer');
                const seedsContainer = document.getElementById('seedsContainer');
                const seedsOutput = document.getElementById('seedsOutput');
                const seedsActionGroup = document.getElementById('seedsActionGroup');
                const seedsPriceCustom = document.getElementById('seedsPriceCustom');
                const seedsQuantity = document.getElementById('seedsQuantity');

                // Track current selection
                let currentItem = '';
                let eachSelected = false;
                let bulkSelected = false;

                function formatPrice(price) {
                    if (!price || price.toLowerCase() === 'negotiable') return 'Negotiable';

                    // If it already has a dollar sign, return as is
                    if (price.startsWith('$')) return price;

                    // If it contains dots, assume it's already formatted with thousands separator
                    if (price.includes('.')) {
                        // Check if it's a valid number with dots as thousands separators
                        const cleaned = price.replace(/\./g, '');
                        if (!isNaN(cleaned) && cleaned !== '') {
                            return '$' + price; // Just add dollar sign
                        }
                    }

                    // If it's a plain number, format it with thousands separator
                    const num = parseFloat(price);
                    if (!isNaN(num)) {
                        // Format without forced decimal places
                        return '$' + num.toLocaleString('de-DE'); // Using German locale for dot thousands separator
                    }

                    // Default fallback
                    return '$' + price;
                }

                function updatePreview() {
                    if (!currentItem) {
                        seedsOutput.textContent = 'Select an item and format to see preview.';
                        return;
                    }

                    const action = seedsActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active').dataset.value;
                    const rawPrice = seedsPriceCustom.value.trim();
                    const price = formatPrice(rawPrice);
                    const quantity = seedsQuantity.value.trim();
                    const priceLabel = action === 'Selling' ? 'Price' : 'Budget';
                    const selectedCategory = seedsCategory.querySelector('.radio.active').dataset.value;
                    const isSeeds = selectedCategory === 'seeds';
                    const isVegetable = selectedCategory === 'vegetables';
                    const itemType = isSeeds ? 'seeds' : (isVegetable ? 'vegetables' : 'fruits');

                    // Handle general seeds case
                    const itemName = currentItem === 'allSeeds' ? 'seeds' : currentItem;
                    // Don't add 'seeds' text if it's general seeds
                    const shouldAddItemType = !(currentItem === 'allSeeds' && itemType === 'seeds');

                    // Add period for "Negotiable" and when either format is selected
                    const needsPeriod = !rawPrice || rawPrice.toLowerCase() === 'negotiable' || eachSelected;

                    // Build the item description with quantity if present
                    function getItemText(includeQuantity = true) {
                        if (quantity && includeQuantity) {
                            return `${quantity} ${itemName}${shouldAddItemType ? ' ' + itemType : ''}`;
                        }
                        return `${itemName}${shouldAddItemType ? ' ' + itemType : ''}`;
                    }

                    // Handle combinations of each and bulk
                    if (eachSelected && bulkSelected) {
                        // Both selected
                        if (quantity) {
                            seedsOutput.textContent = `${action} ${quantity} ${itemName}${shouldAddItemType ? ' ' + itemType : ''} in bulk. ${priceLabel}: ${price} each.`;
                        } else {
                            seedsOutput.textContent = `${action} ${itemName}${shouldAddItemType ? ' ' + itemType : ''} in bulk. ${priceLabel}: ${price} each.`;
                        }
                    } else if (bulkSelected) {
                        // Only bulk selected
                        seedsOutput.textContent = `${action} ${getItemText(false)} in bulk. ${priceLabel}: ${price}${needsPeriod ? '.' : ''}`;
                    } else if (eachSelected) {
                        // Only each selected
                        seedsOutput.textContent = `${action} ${getItemText(true)}. ${priceLabel}: ${price} each.`;
                    } else {
                        // Neither selected
                        seedsOutput.textContent = `${action} ${getItemText(true)}. ${priceLabel}: ${price}${needsPeriod ? '.' : ''}`;
                    }
                }

                // Category toggle
                function updateCategoryDisplay(selectedCategory) {
                    const label = document.getElementById('fruitsVegetablesLabel');
                    if (selectedCategory === 'fruits') {
                        label.textContent = '🌱 Fruits';
                        document.getElementById('fruitsChips').style.display = '';
                        document.getElementById('vegetablesChips').style.display = 'none';
                        document.querySelector('.helper strong').style.display = 'none'; // Hide "Vegetables:" label
                    } else if (selectedCategory === 'vegetables') {
                        label.textContent = '🥦 Vegetables';
                        document.getElementById('fruitsChips').style.display = 'none';
                        document.getElementById('vegetablesChips').style.display = '';
                        document.querySelector('.helper strong').style.display = 'none'; // Hide "Vegetables:" label
                    } else {
                        // For seeds section
                        document.getElementById('fruitsChips').style.display = '';
                        document.getElementById('vegetablesChips').style.display = 'none';
                        document.querySelector('.helper strong').style.display = ''; // Show "Vegetables:" label for seeds view
                    }

                    if (selectedCategory === 'fruits' || selectedCategory === 'vegetables') {
                        fruitsContainer.style.display = '';
                        seedsContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    } else {
                        fruitsContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        seedsContainer.style.display = '';
                    }
                    currentItem = '';
                    updatePreview();
                }

                // Initialize display
                updateCategoryDisplay('fruits');

                // Category toggle
                seedsCategory.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    if (e.target.classList.contains('radio')) {
                        seedsCategory.querySelectorAll('.radio').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('active'));
                        e.target.classList.add('active');
                        updateCategoryDisplay(e.target.dataset.value);
                    }
                });

                // Item selection from both containers
                [fruitsContainer, seedsContainer].forEach(container => {
                    container.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                        if (e.target.classList.contains('chip')) {
                            // If clicking in seeds container, make sure Seeds category is active
                            if (container === seedsContainer) {
                                const seedsRadio = document.querySelector('#seedsCategory [data-value="seeds"]');
                                if (!seedsRadio.classList.contains('active')) {
                                    seedsRadio.click();
                                }
                            }

                            // Only remove active class from chips in the same container
                            container.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                            e.target.classList.add('active');
                            currentItem = e.target.dataset.item;

                            // Check if this is a vegetable chip
                            const isVegetable = e.target.closest('#vegetablesChips') !== null;
                            const selectedCategory = seedsCategory.querySelector('.radio.active').dataset.value;

                            // If we're in the vegetables section, update the category radio button
                            if (isVegetable && selectedCategory !== 'vegetables') {
                                document.querySelector('#seedsCategory [data-value="vegetables"]').click();
                            } else if (!isVegetable && selectedCategory === 'vegetables') {
                                document.querySelector('#seedsCategory [data-value="fruits"]').click();
                            }

                            // Always update preview after selection
                            updatePreview();
                        }
                    });
                });

                // Copy buttons for fruits and vegetables
                document.getElementById('sellFruitsVeggiesBtn').addEventListener('click', function () {
                    const text = 'Selling fruits and vegetables. Price: Negotiable.';
                    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
                    showCopiedFeedback(this, 'Copied!');
                });

                document.getElementById('buyFruitsVeggiesBtn').addEventListener('click', function () {
                    const text = 'Buying fruits and vegetables. Budget: Negotiable.';
                    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
                    showCopiedFeedback(this, 'Copied!');
                });

                // Show feedback when text is copied
                function showCopiedFeedback(button, message) {
                    const originalText = button.textContent;
                    button.textContent = message;
                    button.style.backgroundColor = '#10b981';
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        button.textContent = originalText;
                        button.style.backgroundColor = '';
                    }, 2000);
                }

                // Action toggle
                seedsActionGroup.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    if (e.target.classList.contains('radio')) {
                        seedsActionGroup.querySelectorAll('.radio').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('active'));
                        e.target.classList.add('active');
                        updatePreview();
                    }
                });

                // Each/Bulk buttons
                document.getElementById('seedsEachBtn').addEventListener('click', function () {
                    eachSelected = !eachSelected;
                    this.classList.toggle('active', eachSelected);
                    updatePreview();
                });

                document.getElementById('seedsBulkBtn').addEventListener('click', function () {
                    bulkSelected = !bulkSelected;
                    this.classList.toggle('active', bulkSelected);
                    updatePreview();
                });

                // Price and quantity changes
                seedsPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', updatePreview);
                seedsQuantity.addEventListener('input', updatePreview);

                // Copy button
                document.getElementById('seedsCopyBtn').addEventListener('click', function () {
                    navigator.clipboard.writeText(seedsOutput.textContent);
                });

                // Clear button
                document.getElementById('seedsClearBtn').addEventListener('click', function () {
                    currentItem = '';
                    eachSelected = false;
                    bulkSelected = false;
                    // Remove active class from buttons
                    document.getElementById('seedsEachBtn').classList.remove('active');
                    document.getElementById('seedsBulkBtn').classList.remove('active');
                    seedsPriceCustom.value = '';
                    seedsQuantity.value = '';
                    document.querySelectorAll('#fruitsContainer .chip, #seedsContainer .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    updatePreview();
                });
            });
        </script>

                            style="width:90px;" type="number" />

    <script>
        // Dark Mode Toggle Functionality
        (function () {
            const darkModeToggle = document.getElementById('input');
            const html = document.documentElement;

            // Load saved theme preference
            const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'light';
            if (savedTheme === 'dark') {
                html.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
                if (darkModeToggle) darkModeToggle.checked = true;
            }

            // Toggle dark mode
            function toggleDarkMode() {
                const currentTheme = html.getAttribute('data-theme');
                const newTheme = currentTheme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';

                html.setAttribute('data-theme', newTheme);
                localStorage.setItem('theme', newTheme);

                if (darkModeToggle) {
                    darkModeToggle.checked = newTheme === 'dark';
                }
            }

            // Add event listener when DOM is ready
            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
                    if (darkModeToggle) {
                        darkModeToggle.addEventListener('change', toggleDarkMode);
                    }
                });
            } else {
                if (darkModeToggle) {
                    darkModeToggle.addEventListener('change', toggleDarkMode);
                }
            }
        })();

        // Fullscreen Toggle Functionality
        (function () {
            const fullscreenBtn = document.getElementById('fullscreenBtn');

            if (!fullscreenBtn) return;

            // Check if fullscreen is supported
            const fullscreenSupported = document.fullscreenEnabled ||
                document.webkitFullscreenEnabled ||
                document.mozFullScreenEnabled ||
                document.msFullscreenEnabled;

            // Hide button if fullscreen is not supported
            if (!fullscreenSupported) {
                fullscreenBtn.style.display = 'none';
                return;
            }

            function toggleFullscreen() {
                // Add click animation
                if (fullscreenBtn) {
                    fullscreenBtn.classList.add('clicked');
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        fullscreenBtn.classList.remove('clicked');
                    }, 300);
                }

                if (!document.fullscreenElement &&
                    !document.webkitFullscreenElement &&
                    !document.mozFullScreenElement &&
                    !document.msFullscreenElement) {
                    // Enter fullscreen
                    if (document.documentElement.requestFullscreen) {
                        document.documentElement.requestFullscreen();
                    } else if (document.documentElement.webkitRequestFullscreen) {
                        document.documentElement.webkitRequestFullscreen();
                    } else if (document.documentElement.mozRequestFullScreen) {
                        document.documentElement.mozRequestFullScreen();
                    } else if (document.documentElement.msRequestFullscreen) {
                        document.documentElement.msRequestFullscreen();
                    }
                } else {
                    // Exit fullscreen
                    if (document.exitFullscreen) {
                        document.exitFullscreen();
                    } else if (document.webkitExitFullscreen) {
                        document.webkitExitFullscreen();
                    } else if (document.mozCancelFullScreen) {
                        document.mozCancelFullScreen();
                    } else if (document.msExitFullscreen) {
                        document.msExitFullscreen();
                    }
                }
            }

            fullscreenBtn.addEventListener('click', toggleFullscreen);

            // Update button icon based on fullscreen state
            function updateFullscreenIcon() {
                if (!fullscreenBtn) return;

                if (document.fullscreenElement ||
                    document.webkitFullscreenElement ||
                    document.mozFullScreenElement ||
                    document.msFullscreenElement) {
                    // Currently in fullscreen - show exit icon
                    fullscreenBtn.classList.add('fullscreen-active');
                } else {
                    // Not in fullscreen - show enter icon
                    fullscreenBtn.classList.remove('fullscreen-active');
                }
            }

            // Listen for fullscreen change events
            document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', updateFullscreenIcon);
            document.addEventListener('webkitfullscreenchange', updateFullscreenIcon);
            document.addEventListener('mozfullscreenchange', updateFullscreenIcon);
            document.addEventListener('MSFullscreenChange', updateFullscreenIcon);
        })();

        const actionGroup = document.getElementById('actionGroup');
        const typeGroup = document.getElementById('typeGroup');
        const propNumber = document.getElementById('propNumber');
        const propCount = document.getElementById('propCount');
        const featuresEl = document.getElementById('features');
        const viewsEl = document.getElementById('views');
        const inSelect = document.getElementById('inSelect');
        const inCustom = document.getElementById('inCustom');
        const nearSelect = document.getElementById('nearSelect');
        const nearCustom = document.getElementById('nearCustom');
        const priceSelect = document.getElementById('priceSelect');
        const priceCustom = document.getElementById('priceCustom');
        const priceLabel = document.getElementById('priceLabel');
        const rentHelper = document.getElementById('rentHelper');
        const output = document.getElementById('output');
        // Unified nav toggling (header + sidebar)
        const backBtn = document.getElementById('backBtn');

        function showSection(sectionId) {
            // hide all main sections first
            ['realEstateSection', 'autoSection', 'businessSection', 'workSection', 'datingSection', 'othersSection', 'partySection', 'lookingSection', 'miningSection', 'backpackSection', 'petSection', 'caseSection', 'ticketSection', 'primeSection', 'pickaxeSection', 'fishingSection', 'seedsSection', 'juicesSection', 'tuningSection', 'maskSection', 'shoesSection', 'watchSection', 'pantsSection', 'clothesDepartmentInfo', 'miningResourcesSection', 'accessorySection', 'resourceBarrelSection', 'simLicensePlateSection', 'christmasSection'].forEach(id => {
                const el = document.getElementById(id);
                if (el) el.style.display = 'none';
            });
            const target = document.getElementById(sectionId);
            if (target) target.style.display = '';
            // update header nav active state
            document.querySelectorAll('header nav a span').forEach(s => {
                s.style.background = '#f8fafc';
                s.classList.remove('active');
            });
            document.querySelectorAll('aside#sideNav a span').forEach(s => {
                s.classList.remove('active');
            });
            // mark matching header item
            document.querySelectorAll('header nav a').forEach(a => {
                const span = a.querySelector('span');
                if (span && a.textContent.trim().includes(getLabelFromSection(sectionId))) {
                    span.style.background = '#fff';
                    span.classList.add('active');
                }
            });
            // mark matching side item
            document.querySelectorAll('aside#sideNav a').forEach(a => {
                const s = a.querySelector('span');
                if (a.dataset.section === sectionId && s) {
                    s.classList.add('active');
                }
            });
            // show back button when not on Real Estate
            if (sectionId !== 'realEstateSection') {
                backBtn.style.display = '';
                document.querySelector('main.container').classList.add('with-sidebar');
            } else {
                backBtn.style.display = 'none';
                document.querySelector('main.container').classList.remove('with-sidebar');
            }
        }

        function getLabelFromSection(id) {
            switch (id) {
                case 'realEstateSection':
                    return 'Real Estate';
                case 'autoSection':
                    return 'Auto';
                case 'datingSection':
                    return 'Dating';
                case 'businessSection':
                    return 'Business';
                case 'workSection':
                    return 'Work';
                case 'othersSection':
                    return 'Others';
                case 'clothesDepartmentInfo':
                    return 'Clothes Department';
                default:
                    return '';
            }
        }

        // Attach click handlers to header, sidebar and Others subnav
        document.querySelectorAll('header nav a, aside#sideNav a, #othersSubNav a').forEach(el => {
            el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const section = el.dataset && el.dataset.section ? el.dataset.section : getSectionIdFromLabel(el.textContent || '');
                if (section) showSection(section);
            });
        });

        function getSectionIdFromLabel(label) {
            if (/Real Estate/i.test(label)) return 'realEstateSection';
            if (/Auto/i.test(label)) return 'autoSection';
            if (/Dating/i.test(label)) return 'datingSection';
            if (/Business/i.test(label)) return 'businessSection';
            if (/Work/i.test(label)) return 'workSection';
            if (/Others/i.test(label)) return 'othersSection';
            if (/Clothes Department/i.test(label)) return 'clothesDepartmentInfo';
            return '';
        }

        // make first nav item active on load
        (function () {
            showSection('realEstateSection');
        })();

        // Navigation state tracking
        const navState = {
            mainSection: 'realEstateSection',
            currentSection: 'realEstateSection',
            previousSection: null,
            isSubSection: function (sectionId) {
                return ['partySection', 'lookingSection', 'miningSection', 'backpackSection',
                    'petSection', 'caseSection', 'batterySection', 'ticketSection',
                    'primeSection', 'pickaxeSection', 'fishingSection', 'seedsSection', 'juicesSection', 'tuningSection', 'maskSection', 'shoesSection', 'watchSection', 'pantsSection', 'clothesDepartmentInfo', 'miningResourcesSection', 'resourceBarrelSection', 'simLicensePlateSection'
                ].includes(sectionId);
            }
        };

        // Back button behavior
        backBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const currentSection = navState.currentSection;

            // Check if we're in a clothes section (mask, shoes, watch, pants, accessory, resource barrel, SIM card & license plate)
            const clothesSections = ['maskSection', 'shoesSection', 'watchSection', 'pantsSection', 'accessorySection', 'resourceBarrelSection', 'simLicensePlateSection'];
            if (clothesSections.includes(currentSection)) {
                // If we're in a clothes section, go back to Others
                showSection('othersSection');
            } else if (navState.isSubSection(currentSection)) {
                // If we're in another subsection, go back to Others
                showSection('othersSection');
            } else if (currentSection === 'othersSection') {
                // If we're in Others, go back to Real Estate
                showSection('realEstateSection');
            } else {
                // From any other section, go to Real Estate
                showSection('realEstateSection');
            }
        });

        // Update showSection to track state and handle subsections
        const originalShowSection = showSection;
        showSection = function (sectionId) {
            // Check if it's a clothes section
            const clothesSections = ['maskSection', 'shoesSection', 'watchSection', 'pantsSection', 'accessorySection', 'resourceBarrelSection', 'simLicensePlateSection'];
            if (clothesSections.includes(sectionId)) {
                // Going to a clothes section - store Others as previous
                navState.previousSection = 'othersSection';
            } else if (navState.isSubSection(sectionId)) {
                // Going to another subsection - store Others as previous
                navState.previousSection = 'othersSection';
            } else if (sectionId === 'othersSection') {
                // Going to Others menu - store Real Estate as previous
                navState.previousSection = 'realEstateSection';
            } else {
                // Going to main section - clear history
                navState.previousSection = null;
            }
            navState.currentSection = sectionId;
            originalShowSection(sectionId);
        };

        function partyUpdate(text) {

        function applyHouseNumber(template) {
            const num = (partyHouseNo && partyHouseNo.value.trim()) || '49';
            return template.replace(/№\d+/g, `№${num}`);
        }
        partyChips && partyChips.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            let txt = chip.textContent.trim();

            partyUpdate(txt);
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);
                chip.classList.add('active');
                setTimeout(() => chip.classList.remove('active'), 400);

        });

                alert('Copy failed');
