            partyUpdate('Looking for a party.');
        });

        // Mining chips behavior
        const miningChips = document.getElementById('miningChips');
        const miningOutput = document.getElementById('miningOutput');
        const miningCopyBtn = document.getElementById('miningCopyBtn');
        const miningClearBtn = document.getElementById('miningClearBtn');
        const miningActionGroup = document.getElementById('miningActionGroup');
        const miningCount = document.getElementById('miningCount');
        const miningPriceCustom = document.getElementById('miningPriceCustom');
        const miningFormat = document.getElementById('miningFormat');

        function buildMiningAd() {
            const action = miningActionGroup && miningActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
            const activeChip = miningChips && miningChips.querySelector('.chip.active');
            if (!activeChip) {
                return 'Select an item to generate your ad.';
            }

            const item = activeChip.dataset.item;
            const countRaw = (miningCount && miningCount.value || '').trim();
            const count = countRaw && /^\d+$/.test(countRaw) ? parseInt(countRaw, 10) : undefined;
            const rawPrice = (miningPriceCustom && miningPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = rawPrice ? parseCustomPrice(rawPrice) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

            // Get selected formats
            const formatChips = miningFormat && miningFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip.active') || [];
            const formats = Array.from(formatChips).map(chip => chip.dataset.format);
            const isEach = formats.includes('each');
            const isBulk = formats.includes('bulk');

            let subject = '';
            const isFuelItem = item === 'fuel for resource extraction';
            const isSpecialItem = ['scrap metal', 'top quality metal', 'timber', 'wires'].includes(item);

            // Special handling for scrap metal and top quality metal
            if (item === 'scrap metal' || item === 'top quality metal') {
                if (isBulk) {
                    // For bulk, don't use 'a' and add 'in bulk' at the end
                    subject = count && count > 1 ?
                        `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item} in bulk` :
                        `${capitalize(action)} ${item} in bulk`;
                } else {
                    // For non-bulk, use normal formatting
                    subject = count && count > 1 ?
                        `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item}s` :
                        `${capitalize(action)} a ${item}`;
                }
            }
            // Special handling for bulk format - no articles
            else if (isBulk) {
                if (count && count > 1) {
                    if (isFuelItem || isSpecialItem) {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item}`;
                    } else {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item}s`;
                    }
                } else {
                    // For bulk with single item, no article
                    subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${item}`;
                }
                subject += ' in bulk';
            } else {
                // Normal handling (each format or no format)
                if (count && count > 1) {
                    if (isFuelItem || isSpecialItem) {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item}`;
                    } else {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item}s`;
                    }
                } else {
                    if (isFuelItem || isSpecialItem) {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${item}`;
                    } else {
                        const article = startsWithVowel(item) ? 'an' : 'a';
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${article} ${item}`;
                    }
                }
            }

            // Handle pricing format
            if (isEach) {
                return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${priceText} each.`;
            } else {
                return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            }
        }

        function miningUpdate() {
            if (miningOutput) miningOutput.textContent = buildMiningAd();
            // Update price label
            const action = miningActionGroup && miningActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
            const label = document.getElementById('miningPriceLabel');
            if (label) label.textContent = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
        }

        miningActionGroup && miningActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            [...miningActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            miningUpdate();
        });

        miningChips && miningChips.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            [...miningChips.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            chip.classList.add('active');
            miningUpdate();
        });

        [miningCount, miningPriceCustom].forEach(el => el && el.addEventListener('input', miningUpdate));

        miningFormat && miningFormat.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;

            // Toggle the clicked chip
            chip.classList.toggle('active');

            // If both 'each' and 'bulk' are selected, keep only the most recent selection
            const activeChips = miningFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip.active');
            if (activeChips.length > 1) {
                // Keep only the currently clicked chip active
                miningFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(c => {
                    if (c !== chip) c.classList.remove('active');
                });
            }
            miningUpdate();
        });

        miningCopyBtn && miningCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((miningOutput && miningOutput.textContent) || '');
                miningCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => miningCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });

        miningClearBtn && miningClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (miningCount) miningCount.value = '';
            if (miningPriceCustom) miningPriceCustom.value = '';
            if (miningChips) miningChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            if (miningFormat) miningFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            if (miningActionGroup) {
                [...miningActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                miningActionGroup.children[0].classList.add('active');
            }
            miningUpdate();
        });

        // Looking chips behavior
        const lookingChips = document.getElementById('lookingChips');
        const lookingOutput = document.getElementById('lookingOutput');
        const lookingCopyBtn = document.getElementById('lookingCopyBtn');
        const lookingClearBtn = document.getElementById('lookingClearBtn');
        const lookingHouseNo = document.getElementById('lookingHouseNo');
        const diceBetAmount = document.getElementById('diceBetAmount');
        const diceBetDisplay = document.getElementById('diceBetDisplay');

        function lookingApplyHouseNumber(template) {
            const num = (lookingHouseNo && lookingHouseNo.value.trim()) || '49';
            return template.replace(/№\d+/g, `№${num}`);
        }

        function lookingUpdate(text) {
            if (lookingOutput) lookingOutput.textContent = text || 'Looking for a party.';
        }

        // Handle dice bet amount input
        if (diceBetAmount) {
            diceBetAmount.addEventListener('input', function () {
                const betValue = diceBetAmount.value.trim();
                if (betValue === '') {
                    diceBetDisplay.textContent = 'Bet: Negotiable.';
                } else {
                    // Parse the bet amount similar to other price parsing
                    const parsedBet = parseCustomPrice(betValue);
                    const punctuatedBet = punctuatePrice(parsedBet);

                    // Check if bet exceeds maximum of $10 Million
                    if (parsedBet.startsWith('$')) {
                        // Extract numeric value
                        const numericPart = parsedBet.replace(/^\$/, '').replace(/ Million$/, '');
                        const numericValue = parseFloat(numericPart);

                        // Check if it's in million format
                        if (parsedBet.includes('Million')) {
                            if (numericValue > 10) {
                                diceBetDisplay.textContent = 'Bet: $10 Million. (maximum)';
                                return;
                            }
                        } else {
                            // For regular dollar amounts, convert to check against 10 million
                            const dollarAmount = numericValue;
                            if (dollarAmount > 10000000) { // 10 million
                                diceBetDisplay.textContent = 'Bet: $10 Million. (maximum)';
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    diceBetDisplay.textContent = `Bet: ${punctuatedBet}`;
                }
            });
        }

        lookingChips && lookingChips.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;

            // Special handling for dice chip
            if (chip.contains(diceBetAmount)) {
                const betValue = diceBetAmount.value.trim();
                let betText = 'Bet: Negotiable.';
                if (betValue !== '') {
                    const parsedBet = parseCustomPrice(betValue);
                    const punctuatedBet = punctuatePrice(parsedBet);

                    // Check if bet exceeds maximum of $10 Million
                    if (parsedBet.startsWith('$')) {
                        // Extract numeric value
                        const numericPart = parsedBet.replace(/^\$/, '').replace(/ Million$/, '');
                        const numericValue = parseFloat(numericPart);

                        // Check if it's in million format
                        if (parsedBet.includes('Million')) {
                            if (numericValue > 10) {
                                betText = 'Bet: $10 Million. (maximum)';
                            } else {
                                betText = `Bet: ${punctuatedBet}`;
                            }
                        } else {
                            // For regular dollar amounts, convert to check against 10 million
                            const dollarAmount = numericValue;
                            if (dollarAmount > 10000000) { // 10 million
                                betText = 'Bet: $10 Million. (maximum)';
                            } else {
                                betText = `Bet: ${punctuatedBet}`;
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        betText = `Bet: ${punctuatedBet}`;
                    }
                }
                const text = `Looking to play dice. ${betText}`;
                lookingUpdate(text);
                try {
                    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
                    chip.classList.add('active');
                    setTimeout(() => chip.classList.remove('active'), 400);
                } catch { }
                return;
            }

            let txt = chip.textContent.trim();
            txt = lookingApplyHouseNumber(txt);
            lookingUpdate(txt);
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);
                chip.classList.add('active');
                setTimeout(() => chip.classList.remove('active'), 400);
            } catch { }
        });
        lookingCopyBtn && lookingCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((lookingOutput && lookingOutput.textContent) || '');
                lookingCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => lookingCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        lookingClearBtn && lookingClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            lookingUpdate('Looking for a party.');
            if (diceBetAmount) diceBetAmount.value = '';
            if (diceBetDisplay) diceBetDisplay.textContent = 'Bet: Negotiable.';
        });
        // Battery simple behavior (Selling/Buying, Number, Mode, Copy main)
        const batteryActionGroup = document.getElementById('batteryActionGroup');
        const batteryCount = document.getElementById('batteryCount');
        const batteryModeGroup = document.getElementById('batteryModeGroup');
        const batteryPriceCustom = document.getElementById('batteryPriceCustom');
        const batteryCopyBtn = document.getElementById('batteryCopyBtn');
        const batteryClearBtn = document.getElementById('batteryClearBtn');
        const batteryOutput = document.getElementById('batteryOutput');

        function batteryUpdate(text) {
            if (batteryOutput) batteryOutput.textContent = text || 'Selling a battery. Price: Negotiable.';
        }

        function buildBatteryText(options = {}) {
            // options: mainOnly (boolean) - when true, produce singular default with Negotiable/Budget regardless of other inputs
            const action = (batteryActionGroup && getActiveValue(batteryActionGroup)) || 'Selling';
            if (options.mainOnly) {
                const mainLabel = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
                const priceText = 'Negotiable';
                return `${action} a battery. ${mainLabel}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            }

            const countVal = batteryCount && batteryCount.value ? Number(batteryCount.value) : 0;
            // Get all currently active mode chips
            const activeChips = batteryModeGroup ? [...batteryModeGroup.querySelectorAll('.chip.active')] : [];
            // Get modes from active chips
            const modes = activeChips.map(chip => chip.dataset.mode);
            const priceRaw = batteryPriceCustom ? batteryPriceCustom.value.trim() : '';
            const priceText = parseCustomPrice(priceRaw) || '';
            const label = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

            // Check which modes are selected
            const hasEach = modes.includes('each');
            const hasBulk = modes.includes('bulk');

            // Build sentence according to rules the user requested
            if (!countVal || countVal === 1) {
                // singular
                const pricePart = punctuatePrice(priceText || 'Negotiable');
                // For Negotiable, keep the period; for other prices, remove it
                const cleanPrice = (priceText || 'Negotiable') === 'Negotiable' ? pricePart : (pricePart.endsWith('.') ? pricePart.slice(0, -1) : pricePart);

                // If bulk is selected, show bulk format
                if (hasBulk) {
                    // If both bulk and each are selected, show "each" after price
                    if (hasEach && priceText) {
                        return `${action} batteries in bulk. ${label}: ${cleanPrice} each.`;
                    }
                    return `${action} batteries in bulk. ${label}: ${cleanPrice}`;
                }

                return `${action} a battery. ${label}: ${cleanPrice}`;
            }

            // For count > 1
            const pricePart = punctuatePrice(priceText || 'Negotiable');
            // For Negotiable, keep the period; for other prices, remove it
            const cleanPrice = (priceText || 'Negotiable') === 'Negotiable' ? pricePart : (pricePart.endsWith('.') ? pricePart.slice(0, -1) : pricePart);

            // Handle different mode combinations
            if (hasBulk) {
                // If both bulk and each are selected, show "each" after price
                if (hasEach && priceText) {
                    return `${action} ${countVal} batteries in bulk. ${label}: ${cleanPrice} each.`;
                }
                return `${action} ${countVal} batteries in bulk. ${label}: ${cleanPrice}`;
            } else if (hasEach && priceText) {
                return `${action} ${countVal} batteries. ${label}: ${cleanPrice} each.`;
            } else {
                return `${action} ${countVal} batteries. ${label}: ${cleanPrice}`;
            }
        }

        // Wire interactions: update preview when inputs change
        batteryActionGroup && batteryActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActiveLocal(batteryActionGroup, el);
            batteryUpdate(buildBatteryText());
        });
        batteryModeGroup && batteryModeGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!el) return;
            // Toggle active class on clicked chip
            el.classList.toggle('active');
            batteryUpdate(buildBatteryText());
        });
        batteryCount && batteryCount.addEventListener('input', () => batteryUpdate(buildBatteryText()));
        batteryPriceCustom && batteryPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', () => batteryUpdate(buildBatteryText()));

        batteryCopyBtn && batteryCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const txt = buildBatteryText();
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);
                batteryCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => batteryCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });

        batteryClearBtn && batteryClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            // reset inputs
            if (batteryCount) batteryCount.value = '';
            if (batteryPriceCustom) batteryPriceCustom.value = '';
            // reset modes - clear all selections
            if (batteryModeGroup) {
                [...batteryModeGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            }
            batteryUpdate(`Selling a battery. Price: ${punctuatePrice('Negotiable')}`);
        });

        // initial preview
        batteryUpdate(buildBatteryText());

        // ================= Tuning parts Section =================
        const tuningActionGroup = document.getElementById('tuningActionGroup');
        const tuningPartsChips = document.getElementById('tuningPartsChips');
        const tuningDynamicFields = document.getElementById('tuningDynamicFields');
        const tuningPriceCustom = document.getElementById('tuningPriceCustom');
        const tuningPriceLabel = document.getElementById('tuningPriceLabel');
        const tuningOutput = document.getElementById('tuningOutput');
        const tuningCopyBtn = document.getElementById('tuningCopyBtn');
        const tuningClearBtn = document.getElementById('tuningClearBtn');

        const tuningPartsData = {}; // {partName: {quality: 'high', count: 1}}

        function tuningRenderDynamicFields() {
            if (!tuningDynamicFields) return;
            const selectedParts = [...tuningPartsChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active')].map(c => c.dataset.part);
            tuningDynamicFields.innerHTML = '';

            if (selectedParts.length === 0) return;

            const grid = document.createElement('div');
            grid.className = 'grid';

            selectedParts.forEach(part => {
                const field = document.createElement('div');
                field.className = 'field sm-4';
                field.innerHTML = `
        <label>${capitalize(part)} Quality</label>
        <div class="radio-group" data-part="${part}">
          <div class="radio" data-quality="low">Low</div>
          <div class="radio" data-quality="medium">Medium</div>
          <div class="radio active" data-quality="high">High</div>
        </div>
        <input type="number" min="1" placeholder="Count (default: 1)" 
               style="margin-top:8px;" data-part="${part}" class="tuning-count-input"/>
      `;
                grid.appendChild(field);

                // Initialize data
                if (!tuningPartsData[part]) {
                    tuningPartsData[part] = {
                        quality: 'high',
                        count: 1
                    };
                }
            });

            tuningDynamicFields.appendChild(grid);

            // Add event listeners
            grid.querySelectorAll('.radio-group').forEach(group => {
                group.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    const radio = e.target.closest('.radio');
                    if (!radio) return;
                    const part = group.dataset.part;
                    [...group.children].forEach(r => r.classList.remove('active'));
                    radio.classList.add('active');
                    tuningPartsData[part].quality = radio.dataset.quality;
                    tuningUpdate();
                });
            });

            grid.querySelectorAll('.tuning-count-input').forEach(input => {
                input.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                    const part = e.target.dataset.part;
                    const val = parseInt(e.target.value) || 1;
                    tuningPartsData[part].count = val;
                    tuningUpdate();
                });
            });
        }

        function buildTuningAd() {
            const action = tuningActionGroup && tuningActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
            const selectedParts = [...tuningPartsChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active')].map(c => c.dataset.part);

            if (selectedParts.length === 0) {
                const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
                // Show appropriate message based on action and quality
                if (action === 'Selling') {
                    return 'Selling high quality tuning parts. Price: Negotiable.';
                } else {
                    return 'Buying high quality tuning parts. Budget: Negotiable.';
                }
            }

            const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const priceRaw = (tuningPriceCustom && tuningPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = priceRaw ? parseCustomPrice(priceRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            const finalPrice = punctuatePrice(priceText);

            // Build parts array with quality and count
            const parts = selectedParts.map(part => ({
                name: part,
                quality: tuningPartsData[part]?.quality || 'high',
                count: tuningPartsData[part]?.count || 1
            }));

            // Group by quality
            const qualityGroups = {};
            parts.forEach(p => {
                if (!qualityGroups[p.quality]) qualityGroups[p.quality] = [];
                qualityGroups[p.quality].push(p);
            });

            const uniqueQualities = Object.keys(qualityGroups);

            // CASE 1: All same quality
            if (uniqueQualities.length === 1) {
                const quality = uniqueQualities[0];
                const allParts = qualityGroups[quality];

                // Check if all have same count
                const counts = allParts.map(p => p.count);
                const allSameCount = counts.every(c => c === counts[0]);
                const singleCount = counts[0];

                if (allParts.length === 1) {
                    const p = allParts[0];
                    if (p.count === 1) {
                        return `${capitalize(action)} ${quality} quality ${p.name} tuning. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
                    } else {
                        return `${capitalize(action)} ${p.count} ${quality} quality ${p.name} tunings. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
                    }
                } else if (allSameCount && singleCount === 1) {
                    // e.g., "Selling high quality tires and transmission tunings"
                    const partNames = allParts.map(p => p.name);
                    const joined = joinPartNames(partNames);
                    return `${capitalize(action)} ${quality} quality ${joined} tunings. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
                } else if (allSameCount && singleCount > 1) {
                    // e.g., "Selling 2 medium quality engine tunings"
                    const partNames = allParts.map(p => p.name);
                    const joined = joinPartNames(partNames);
                    return `${capitalize(action)} ${singleCount} ${quality} quality ${joined} tunings. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
                } else {
                    // Different counts, list individually
                    const partStrs = allParts.map(p => {
                        if (p.count === 1) return `${quality} quality ${p.name}`;
                        return `${p.count} ${quality} quality ${p.name}`;
                    });
                    const joined = joinPartNames(partStrs);
                    return `${capitalize(action)} ${joined} tunings. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
                }
            }

            // CASE 2: Two different qualities
            if (uniqueQualities.length === 2) {
                const q1 = uniqueQualities[0];
                const q2 = uniqueQualities[1];
                const g1 = qualityGroups[q1];
                const g2 = qualityGroups[q2];

                // Determine which has more parts
                let majorGroup, minorGroup, majorQuality, minorQuality;
                if (g1.length > g2.length) {
                    majorGroup = g1;
                    majorQuality = q1;
                    minorGroup = g2;
                    minorQuality = q2;
                } else if (g2.length > g1.length) {
                    majorGroup = g2;
                    majorQuality = q2;
                    minorGroup = g1;
                    minorQuality = q1;
                } else {
                    // Equal sizes, just use order
                    majorGroup = g1;
                    majorQuality = q1;
                    minorGroup = g2;
                    minorQuality = q2;
                }

                // Check if major group all have count 1 and same, minor has count 1
                const majorCounts = majorGroup.map(p => p.count);
                const minorCounts = minorGroup.map(p => p.count);
                const majorAllOne = majorCounts.every(c => c === 1);
                const minorAllOne = minorCounts.every(c => c === 1);
                const majorSameCount = majorCounts.every(c => c === majorCounts[0]);

                if (majorGroup.length === 2 && minorGroup.length === 1 && majorAllOne && minorAllOne) {
                    // e.g., "Selling medium quality transmission tuning and high quality tires and suspension tunings"
                    const minorPart = minorGroup[0].name;
                    const majorNames = joinPartNames(majorGroup.map(p => p.name));
                    return `${capitalize(action)} ${minorQuality} quality ${minorPart} tuning and ${majorQuality} quality ${majorNames} tunings. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
                } else if (majorGroup.length === 2 && minorGroup.length === 1 && majorSameCount && majorCounts[0] > 1) {
                    // e.g., "Selling medium quality engine and 2 high quality transmission and suspension tunings"
                    const minorPart = minorGroup[0].name;
                    const minorCount = minorGroup[0].count;
                    const majorNames = joinPartNames(majorGroup.map(p => p.name));
                    const majorCount = majorCounts[0];
                    if (minorCount === 1) {
                        return `${capitalize(action)} ${minorQuality} quality ${minorPart} and ${majorCount} ${majorQuality} quality ${majorNames} tunings. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
                    }
                } else if (majorGroup.length === 2 && minorGroup.length === 1) {
                    // Different counts -> generic "tuning parts"
                    const minorPart = minorGroup[0].name;
                    const minorCount = minorGroup[0].count;
                    let minorStr = minorCount === 1 ? `${minorQuality} quality ${minorPart} tuning` : `${minorCount} ${minorQuality} quality ${minorPart} tunings`;
                    return `${capitalize(action)} ${majorQuality} quality tuning parts and ${minorStr}. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
                }

                // Default: list all with quality
                const allPartStrs = parts.map(p => {
                    if (p.count === 1) return `${p.quality} quality ${p.name}`;
                    return `${p.count} ${p.quality} quality ${p.name}`;
                });
                const joined = joinPartNames(allPartStrs);
                return `${capitalize(action)} ${joined} tunings. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
            }

            // CASE 3: Three different qualities
            if (uniqueQualities.length === 3) {
                const allPartStrs = parts.map(p => {
                    if (p.count === 1) return `${p.quality} quality ${p.name}`;
                    return `${p.count} ${p.quality} quality ${p.name}`;
                });
                const joined = joinPartNames(allPartStrs);
                return `${capitalize(action)} ${joined} tunings. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
            }

            // Fallback
            return `${capitalize(action)} tuning parts. ${priceWord}: ${finalPrice}`;
        }

        function joinPartNames(names) {
            if (names.length === 1) return names[0];
            if (names.length === 2) return names.join(' and ');
            const last = names[names.length - 1];
            return names.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + last;
        }

        function tuningUpdate() {
            if (tuningOutput) tuningOutput.textContent = buildTuningAd();
            const action = tuningActionGroup && tuningActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
            if (tuningPriceLabel) tuningPriceLabel.textContent = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
        }

        tuningActionGroup && tuningActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            [...tuningActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            tuningUpdate();
        });

        tuningPartsChips && tuningPartsChips.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            chip.classList.toggle('active');
            const part = chip.dataset.part;
            if (!chip.classList.contains('active')) {
                delete tuningPartsData[part];
            }
            tuningRenderDynamicFields();
            tuningUpdate();
        });

        tuningPriceCustom && tuningPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', tuningUpdate);

        tuningCopyBtn && tuningCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((tuningOutput && tuningOutput.textContent) || '');
                tuningCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => tuningCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });

        tuningClearBtn && tuningClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (tuningPriceCustom) tuningPriceCustom.value = '';
            if (tuningPartsChips) tuningPartsChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            Object.keys(tuningPartsData).forEach(k => delete tuningPartsData[k]);
            if (tuningActionGroup) {
                [...tuningActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                tuningActionGroup.children[0].classList.add('active');
            }
            tuningRenderDynamicFields();
            tuningUpdate();
        });

        // ================= Mask Section Formatter =================
        const maskActionGroup = document.getElementById('maskActionGroup');
        const maskGenderGroup = document.getElementById('maskGenderGroup');
        const maskItem1 = document.getElementById('maskItem1');
        const maskItem2 = document.getElementById('maskItem2');
        const maskItemType1Qty = document.getElementById('maskItemType1Qty');
        const maskItemType1Desc = document.getElementById('maskItemType1Desc');
        const maskItemType2Qty = document.getElementById('maskItemType2Qty');
        const maskItemType2Desc = document.getElementById('maskItemType2Desc');
        const maskPriceCustom = document.getElementById('maskPriceCustom');
        const maskPriceLabel = document.getElementById('maskPriceLabel');
        const maskOutput = document.getElementById('maskOutput');
        const maskCopyBtn = document.getElementById('maskCopyBtn');
        const maskClearBtn = document.getElementById('maskClearBtn');

        function buildMaskText() {
            const action = (maskActionGroup && maskActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            const gender = (maskGenderGroup && maskGenderGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || '';
            const item1 = (maskItem1 && maskItem1.value.trim()) || '';
            const item2 = (maskItem2 && maskItem2.value.trim()) || '';
            const itemType1Qty = (maskItemType1Qty && maskItemType1Qty.value.trim()) || '';
            const itemType2Qty = (maskItemType2Qty && maskItemType2Qty.value.trim()) || '';
            const label = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const priceRaw = maskPriceCustom ? maskPriceCustom.value.trim() : '';
            const priceText = priceRaw ? parseCustomPrice(priceRaw) : 'Negotiable';

            // Build item description
            let itemDesc = '';

            // Use item types with numbers if provided (for "of type" format)
            // Use regular mask items with quantity from mask item type fields
            if (item1 && item2) {
                // Check if we should apply "of type" format to regular items
                if (itemType1Qty && item1.toLowerCase().includes('mask')) {
                    // Use item1 with itemType1Qty
                    itemDesc = `${item1} of type ${itemType1Qty}`;
                    // Also check if we can use item2 with itemType2Qty
                    if (itemType2Qty && item2.toLowerCase().includes('mask')) {
                        itemDesc += ` and ${item2} of type ${itemType2Qty}`;
                    } else if (itemType2Qty) {
                        itemDesc += ` and ${itemType2Qty}x ${item2}`;
                    } else {
                        // Just add item2 normally
                        itemDesc += ` and ${item2}`;
                    }
                } else if (itemType1Qty) {
                    // For non-mask items with quantity
                    itemDesc = `${itemType1Qty}x ${item1}`;
                    if (itemType2Qty) {
                        itemDesc += ` and ${itemType2Qty}x ${item2}`;
                    } else {
                        itemDesc += ` and ${item2}`;
                    }
                } else {
                    itemDesc = `${item1} and ${item2}`;
                }
            } else if (item1) {
                // Check if we should apply "of type" format to regular items
                if (itemType1Qty && item1.toLowerCase().includes('mask')) {
                    itemDesc = `${item1} of type ${itemType1Qty}`;
                } else if (itemType1Qty) {
                    // For non-mask items with quantity
                    itemDesc = `${itemType1Qty}x ${item1}`;
                } else {
                    itemDesc = item1;
                }
            } else if (item2) {
                // Check if we should apply "of type" format to regular items
                if (itemType2Qty && item2.toLowerCase().includes('mask')) {
                    itemDesc = `${item2} of type ${itemType2Qty}`;
                } else if (itemType2Qty) {
                    // For non-mask items with quantity
                    itemDesc = `${itemType2Qty}x ${item2}`;
                } else if (itemType1Qty && item2.toLowerCase().includes('mask')) {
                    // Fallback to itemType1Qty for item2 if itemType2Qty is not available
                    itemDesc = `${item2} of type ${itemType1Qty}`;
                } else if (itemType1Qty) {
                    // For non-mask items with quantity
                    itemDesc = `${itemType1Qty}x ${item2}`;
                } else {
                    itemDesc = item2;
                }
            } else {
                itemDesc = 'mask';
            }

            // Add gender specification only if selected
            const genderText = gender ? ` for ${gender.toLowerCase()}` : '';

            return `${capitalize(action)} ${itemDesc}${genderText}. ${label}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function maskUpdate() {
            if (maskOutput) maskOutput.textContent = buildMaskText();
        }

        // Validate mask quantity to ensure only 3-digit numbers
        function validateMaskQuantity(input) {
            let value = input.value;
            // Remove any non-digit characters
            value = value.replace(/\D/g, '');
            // Limit to 3 digits
            if (value.length > 3) {
                value = value.substring(0, 3);
            }
            // Ensure value is between 1 and 999
            if (value !== '') {
                const numValue = parseInt(value, 10);
                if (numValue < 1) {
                    value = '1';
                } else if (numValue > 999) {
                    value = '999';
                }
            }
            input.value = value;
        }

        maskActionGroup && maskActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            [...maskActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            if (maskPriceLabel) maskPriceLabel.textContent = el.dataset.value === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            maskUpdate();
        });
        maskGenderGroup && maskGenderGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            [...maskGenderGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            maskUpdate();
        });
        maskItem1 && maskItem1.addEventListener('input', maskUpdate);
        maskItem1 && maskItem1.addEventListener('change', maskUpdate);
        maskItem2 && maskItem2.addEventListener('input', maskUpdate);
        maskItem2 && maskItem2.addEventListener('change', maskUpdate);
        maskItemType1Qty && maskItemType1Qty.addEventListener('input', maskUpdate);
        maskItemType1Qty && maskItemType1Qty.addEventListener('change', maskUpdate);
        // maskItemType1Desc removed
        maskItemType2Qty && maskItemType2Qty.addEventListener('input', maskUpdate);
        maskItemType2Qty && maskItemType2Qty.addEventListener('change', maskUpdate);
        // maskItemType2Desc removed
        maskPriceCustom && maskPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', maskUpdate);

        maskCopyBtn && maskCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((maskOutput && maskOutput.textContent) || '');
                maskCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => maskCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        maskClearBtn && maskClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (maskItem1) maskItem1.value = '';
            if (maskItem2) maskItem2.value = '';
            if (maskItemType1Qty) maskItemType1Qty.value = '';
            if (maskItemType2Qty) maskItemType2Qty.value = '';
            if (maskPriceCustom) maskPriceCustom.value = '';
            if (maskActionGroup) {
                [...maskActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                maskActionGroup.children[0].classList.add('active');
            }
            if (maskGenderGroup) {
                [...maskGenderGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            }
            if (maskPriceLabel) maskPriceLabel.textContent = 'Price';
            maskUpdate();
        });

        maskUpdate();

        // ================= SHOES Section Formatter =================
        const shoesActionGroup = document.getElementById('shoesActionGroup');
        const shoesGenderGroup = document.getElementById('shoesGenderGroup');
        const shoesItem = document.getElementById('shoesItem');
        const shoesItemType1 = document.getElementById('shoesItemType1');
        const shoesItemType2 = document.getElementById('shoesItemType2');
        const shoesPriceCustom = document.getElementById('shoesPriceCustom');
        const shoesPriceLabel = document.getElementById('shoesPriceLabel');
        const shoesOutput = document.getElementById('shoesOutput');
        const shoesCopyBtn = document.getElementById('shoesCopyBtn');
        const shoesClearBtn = document.getElementById('shoesClearBtn');

        function buildShoesText() {
            const action = (shoesActionGroup && shoesActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            const gender = (shoesGenderGroup && shoesGenderGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || '';
            let item = (shoesItem && shoesItem.value.trim()) || 'shoes';
            const label = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const priceRaw = shoesPriceCustom ? shoesPriceCustom.value.trim() : '';
            const priceText = priceRaw ? parseCustomPrice(priceRaw) : 'Negotiable';

            // Handle item types with quantities (similar to top/hoodie section)
            const itemType1Qty = (shoesItemType1 && shoesItemType1.value.trim()) || '';
            const itemType2Qty = (shoesItemType2 && shoesItemType2.value.trim()) || '';

            // Build item description based on quantities
            let itemDesc = item;

            // Check if item is in "Nx item" format and convert to "item of type N" for shoes items
            // Since this is the SHOES section, all items are considered shoes items
            const nxMatch = item.match(/^([0-9]+)x\s+(.+)$/i);
            if (nxMatch) {
                const quantity = nxMatch[1];
                const baseItem = nxMatch[2];
                itemDesc = `${baseItem} of type ${quantity}`;
            } else {
                // Use the type fields if no Nx format is detected
                if (itemType1Qty) {
                    itemDesc = `${item} of type ${itemType1Qty}`;
                } else if (itemType2Qty) {
                    // Only itemType2 is provided
                    itemDesc = `${item} of type ${itemType2Qty}`;
                }
            }

            // Add gender specification only if selected
            const genderText = gender ? ` for ${gender.toLowerCase()}` : '';

            return `${capitalize(action)} ${itemDesc}${genderText}. ${label}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function buildWatchText() {
            const action = (watchActionGroup && watchActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            let item = (watchItem && watchItem.value.trim()) || 'watch';
            const label = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const priceRaw = watchPriceCustom ? watchPriceCustom.value.trim() : '';
            const priceText = priceRaw ? parseCustomPrice(priceRaw) : 'Negotiable';

            // Handle item types with quantities (similar to top/hoodie section)
            const itemType1Qty = (watchItemType1 && watchItemType1.value.trim()) || '';
            const itemType2Qty = (watchItemType2 && watchItemType2.value.trim()) || '';

            // Build item description based on quantities
            let itemDesc = item;
            if (itemType1Qty && item.toLowerCase().includes('watch')) {
                itemDesc = `${item} of type ${itemType1Qty}`;
            } else if (itemType1Qty) {
                // For non-watch items with quantity
                itemDesc = `${itemType1Qty}x ${item}`;
            }

            if (itemType2Qty && item.toLowerCase().includes('watch')) {
                itemDesc = `${item} of type ${itemType1Qty} and ${itemType2Qty}`;
            } else if (itemType2Qty) {
                // For non-watch items with quantity
                if (itemType1Qty) {
                    itemDesc = `${itemType1Qty}x and ${itemType2Qty}x ${item}`;
                } else {
                    itemDesc = `${itemType2Qty}x ${item}`;
                }
            }

            return `${capitalize(action)} ${itemDesc}. ${label}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function buildTopHoodieText() {
            const action = (topHoodieActionGroup && topHoodieActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            const gender = (topHoodieGenderGroup && topHoodieGenderGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || '';
            const color = (topHoodieColorGroup && topHoodieColorGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || '';
            let item = (topHoodieItem && topHoodieItem.value.trim()) || 'top/hoodie';
            const label = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const priceRaw = topHoodiePriceCustom ? topHoodiePriceCustom.value.trim() : '';
            const priceText = priceRaw ? parseCustomPrice(priceRaw) : 'Negotiable';

            // Handle item types with quantities (similar to pants section)
            const itemType1Qty = (topHoodieItemType1 && topHoodieItemType1.value.trim()) || '';
            const itemType2Qty = (topHoodieItemType2 && topHoodieItemType2.value.trim()) || '';

            // Build item description based on quantities and color
            let itemDesc = item;

            // Add color prefix if selected
            if (color) {
                itemDesc = `${color} ${itemDesc}`;
            }

            // Add gender suffix if selected
            if (gender) {
                itemDesc = `${itemDesc} for ${gender.toLowerCase()}`;
            }

            // Check if item is in "Nx item" format and convert to "item of type N" for top/hoodie items
            // Since this is the TOP/HOODIE section, all items are considered top/hoodie items
            const nxMatch = item.match(/^([0-9]+)x\s+(.+)$/i);
            if (nxMatch) {
                const quantity = nxMatch[1];
                const baseItem = nxMatch[2];
                itemDesc = `${baseItem} of type ${quantity}`;
            } else {
                // Use the type fields if no Nx format is detected
                if (itemType1Qty) {
                    itemDesc = `${item} of type ${itemType1Qty}`;
                } else if (itemType2Qty) {
                    // Only itemType2 is provided
                    itemDesc = `${item} of type ${itemType2Qty}`;
                }
            }

            return `${capitalize(action)} ${itemDesc}. ${label}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        const copyBtn = document.getElementById('copyBtn');
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearBtn');

        function setActive(container, el) {
            [...container.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            update();
        }
        // Local variant that doesn't trigger the global update(), used by small sub-sections
        function setActiveLocal(container, el) {
            if (!container || !el) return;
            [...container.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
        }

        function getActiveValue(container) {
            const active = container.querySelector('.active');
            return active ? active.dataset.value : '';
        }

        actionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActive(actionGroup, el);
            const action = el.dataset.value;
            if (action === 'Buying' || action === 'Looking to rent') {
                priceLabel.textContent = 'Budget';
                rentHelper.style.display = 'none';
            } else if (action === 'Renting out') {
                priceLabel.textContent = 'Rent';
                rentHelper.style.display = '';
            } else {
                priceLabel.textContent = 'Price';
                rentHelper.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });

        typeGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActive(typeGroup, el);
        });

        featuresEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            chip.classList.toggle('active');
            update();
        });

        [propNumber, propCount, viewsEl].forEach(el => el.addEventListener('input', update));
        priceSelect.addEventListener('change', update);
        priceCustom.addEventListener('input', update);

        [inSelect, nearSelect].forEach(sel => {
            sel.addEventListener('change', () => {
                const isCustom = sel.value === '__custom';
                const box = sel === inSelect ? inCustom : nearCustom;
                box.style.display = isCustom ? '' : 'none';
                update();
            });
        });
        [inCustom, nearCustom].forEach(el => el.addEventListener('input', update));

        function titleCaseLocation(value) {
            if (!value) return '';
            // Special cases: keep these phrases in lowercase
            if (value.toLowerCase() === 'the beach market') return 'the beach market';
            if (value.toLowerCase() === 'the ghetto') return 'the ghetto';
            if (value.toLowerCase() === 'the city') return 'the city';
            return value.split(/\s+/).map(w => w ? w[0].toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase() : '').join(' ');
        }

        function getLocationValue(selectEl, customEl) {
            if (!selectEl) return '';
            if (selectEl.value === '__custom') return titleCaseLocation(customEl.value.trim());
            return titleCaseLocation(selectEl.value.trim());
        }

        function buildFeaturesList(type) {
            const order = ['garden', 'gs2', 'gs5', 'gs9', 'gs25', 'wh3', 'wh4', 'wh5', 'customInterior', 'insurance', 'helipad', 'swimmingPool', 'tennisCourt', 'longDriveway', 'spaciousBackyard'];
            const activeKeys = [...featuresEl.querySelectorAll('.chip.active')].map(c => c.dataset.key);
            const picked = order.filter(k => activeKeys.includes(k));

            // Apartments cannot have insurance
            if (type === 'apartment') {
                const idx = picked.indexOf('insurance');
                if (idx !== -1) picked.splice(idx, 1);
            }

            const parts = [];
            if (picked.includes('garden')) parts.push('a garden');
            const garageMap = {
                gs2: '2 g.s.',
                gs5: '5 g.s.',
                gs9: '9 g.s.',
                gs25: '25 g.s.'
            };
            ['gs2', 'gs5', 'gs9', 'gs25'].forEach(k => {
                if (picked.includes(k)) parts.push(garageMap[k]);
            });
            const whMap = {
                wh3: '3 w.h.',
                wh4: '4 w.h.',
                wh5: '5 w.h.'
            };
            ['wh3', 'wh4', 'wh5'].forEach(k => {
                if (picked.includes(k)) parts.push(whMap[k]);
            });
            if (picked.includes('customInterior')) parts.push('custom interior');
            if (picked.includes('insurance')) parts.push('insurance');
            if (picked.includes('helipad')) parts.push('helipad');
            if (picked.includes('swimmingPool')) parts.push('swimming pool');
            if (picked.includes('tennisCourt')) parts.push('tennis court');
            if (picked.includes('longDriveway')) parts.push('long driveway');
            if (picked.includes('spaciousBackyard')) parts.push('spacious backyard');

            return parts;
        }

        function joinFeatures(parts) {
            // Return the " with ..." segment WITHOUT a trailing period,
            // so tokens like "25 g.s." or "3 w.h." don't produce "..".
            if (parts.length === 0) return '';
            if (parts.length === 1) return ' with ' + parts[0];
            if (parts.length === 2) return ' with ' + parts[0] + ' and ' + parts[1];
            const last = parts.pop();
            return ' with ' + parts.join(', ') + ' and ' + last;
        }

        function parseCustomPrice(text) {
            const s = (text || '').trim();
            if (!s) return '';
            // 1) Dotted thousands like 100.000 or $250.000 -> keep format and ensure $ prefix
            if (/^\$?\d{1,3}(?:\.\d{3})+$/.test(s)) {
                return s.startsWith('$') ? s : `$${s}`;
            }
            // 2) Already specified with $ or Million -> normalize Million case
            if (/\$|million/i.test(s)) return normalizeMillionCase(s);
            // 3) Decimals with ONE or TWO digits after dot -> Million (e.g., 12.5 => $12.5 Million, 90.25 => $90.25 Million)
            if (/^\d+(?:\.\d{1,2})$/.test(s)) {
                return `$${s} Million`;
            }
            // 4) Pure numeric handling for integers
            const num = Number(s.replace(/[\,\s]/g, ''));
            if (!Number.isNaN(num)) {
                if (num >= 100) {
                    // Convert to dotted thousands (e.g., 250000 -> $250.000)
                    return `$${String(Math.trunc(num)).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.')}`;
                }
                if (num >= 1) {
                    // 1..99 -> Millions
                    return `$${String(Math.trunc(num))} Million`;
                }
            }
            return s;
        }

        function getPriceText(action) {
            // If custom is filled, it overrides presets
            const vCustom = (priceCustom.value || '').trim();
            if (vCustom) {
                return parseCustomPrice(vCustom);
            }
            // Otherwise use preset value or Negotiable
            if (priceSelect.value) {
                return priceSelect.value;
            }
            return 'Negotiable';
        }

        function normalizeMillionCase(s) {
            return s.replace(/million/ig, 'Million');
        }

        function punctuatePrice(text) {
            const t = (text || '').trim();
            if (!t) return text;
            if (/\.$/.test(t)) return text; // already has trailing period
            // Special case: Add period after "Million" even if it starts with $
            if (/Million$/i.test(t)) return t + '.';
            // Don't add period to prices that start with currency symbols like $
            if (/^\$/.test(t)) return t;
            // Add period to "Negotiable" and other text prices
            return t + '.';
        }

        function formatNumber(n) {
            // format without decimals, with grouping per dot as in screenshots (e.g., 100.000)
            // but for millions, typical style is plain (e.g., 300). We'll keep plain digits.
            return String(Math.trunc(n));
        }

        function buildSentence() {
            let action = getActiveValue(actionGroup);
            let type = getActiveValue(typeGroup);
            if (type === 'Casino penthouse') type = 'Casino penthouse';

            const numberRaw = propNumber.value.trim();
            const hasNumber = numberRaw !== '';
            let count = propCount.value ? parseInt(propCount.value, 10) : undefined;
            if (hasNumber) count = undefined;

            const views = viewsEl.value;
            const inLoc = getLocationValue(inSelect, inCustom);
            const nearLoc = getLocationValue(nearSelect, nearCustom);
            const priceText = getPriceText(action);

            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying' || action === 'Looking to rent') ? 'Budget' : (action === 'Renting out' ? 'Rent' : 'Price');

            let subject = '';
            const baseTypeWord = type;
            if (hasNumber) {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${baseTypeWord} №${numberRaw}`;
            } else if (count && count > 1) {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${pluralize(baseTypeWord)}`;
            } else {
                if (baseTypeWord === 'Casino penthouse') {
                    subject = `${capitalize(action)} Casino penthouse`;
                } else {
                    const article = startsWithVowel(baseTypeWord) ? 'an' : 'a';
                    subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${article} ${baseTypeWord}`;
                }
            }

            const featureParts = buildFeaturesList(type);
            if (views) featureParts.push(views);

            let sentence = subject;
            sentence += joinFeatures(featureParts);

            const locBits = [];
            if (inLoc) locBits.push(`in ${inLoc}`);
            if (nearLoc) locBits.push(`near ${nearLoc}`);
            if (locBits.length) {
                sentence += ' ' + locBits.join(' ');
            }

            if (!sentence.endsWith('.')) sentence += '.'; // ensure exactly one period

            const priceLine = `${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            return `${sentence} ${priceLine}`.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/\s\./g, '.');
        }

        function update() {
            output.textContent = buildSentence();
        }

        function capitalize(s) {
            return s ? s[0].toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : s;
        }

        function pluralize(s) {
            if (s === 'house') return 'houses';
            if (s === 'apartment') return 'apartments';
            if (s === 'mansion') return 'mansions';
            if (s === 'Casino penthouse') return 'Casino penthouses';
            return s + 's';
        }

        function startsWithVowel(s) {
            return /^[aeiou]/i.test(s);
        }

        copyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(output.textContent.trim());
                copyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => (copyBtn.textContent = 'Copy'), 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });

        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            [propNumber].forEach(el => el.value = '');
            viewsEl.value = '';
            propCount.value = '';
            featuresEl.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            inSelect.value = '';
            nearSelect.value = '';
            inCustom.value = '';
            nearCustom.value = '';
            inCustom.style.display = 'none';
            nearCustom.style.display = 'none';
            priceSelect.value = '';
            priceCustom.value = '';
            setActive(actionGroup, actionGroup.children[0]);
            setActive(typeGroup, typeGroup.children[0]);
            update();
        });

        update();

        // Million quick-set helpers
        function bindMillionQuickSet(inputEl, buttonEl) {
            if (!inputEl || !buttonEl) return;
            buttonEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const v = inputEl.value.trim();
                if (!v) return;
                const n = Number(v);
                if (Number.isNaN(n)) return;
                const clamped = Math.max(1, Math.min(300, Math.trunc(n)));
                // Put formatted Million text into sibling custom input if present
                const wrapper = buttonEl.closest('.field');
                const custom = wrapper && wrapper.querySelector('input[type="text"]');
                if (custom) {
                    custom.value = `$${clamped} Million.`;
                }
                // Trigger updates depending on which field
                if (custom === priceCustom) update();
                if (custom === autoPriceCustom) autoUpdate();
                if (custom === bizPriceCustom) bizUpdate();
                if (custom === workPriceCustom) workUpdate();
                if (custom === backpackPriceCustom) backpackUpdate();
                if (custom === petPriceCustom) petUpdate();
                if (custom === shoulderPetsPriceCustom) updatePreview();
            });
        }

        function autoFormatMillions(v) {
            if (!v) return '';
            if (/\$|million/i.test(v)) return v.replace(/million/ig, 'Million');
            const n = Number(v.replace(/[,\s]/g, ''));
            if (!Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0) return `$${String(Math.trunc(n))} Million.`;
            return v;
        }

        function autoGetPriceText(action) {
            const c = (autoPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            if (c) {
                return parseCustomPrice(c);
            }
            if (autoPriceSelect.value) return autoPriceSelect.value;
            return 'Negotiable';
        }

        function q(n) {
            n = (n || '').trim();
            if (!n) return '';
            return '"' + n + '"';
        }

        function autoBuildFeatures() {
            const order = ['fullConfig', 'partialConfig', 'visualUpgrades', 'luminousWheels', 'insurance', 'turboKit', 'driftKit'];
            const labels = {
                fullConfig: 'full configuration',
                partialConfig: 'partial configuration',
                visualUpgrades: 'visual upgrades',
                luminousWheels: 'luminous wheels',
                insurance: 'insurance',
                turboKit: 'turbo kit',
                driftKit: 'drift kit'
            };
            const active = [...autoFeatures.querySelectorAll('.chip.active')].map(c => c.dataset.key);
            if (active.includes('fullConfig')) {
                const i = active.indexOf('partialConfig');
                if (i !== -1) active.splice(i, 1);
            }
            const parts = [];
            order.forEach(k => {
                if (active.includes(k)) parts.push(labels[k]);
            });
            return parts;
        }

        function autoJoin(parts) {
            if (parts.length === 0) return '';
            if (parts.length === 1) return ' with ' + parts[0];
            if (parts.length === 2) return ' with ' + parts[0] + ' and ' + parts[1];
            const last = parts.pop();
            return ' with ' + parts.join(', ') + ' and ' + last;
        }

        function autoBuild() {
            const action = getActiveAuto(autoActionGroup);
            const name = q(vehicleName.value);
            const trade = (tradeTarget.value || '').trim();
            const feats = autoBuildFeatures();
            const monoVal = (monoType.value || '').trim();
            const priceWord = action === 'Buying' || action === 'Renting' ? 'Budget' : (action === 'Renting out' ? 'Rent' : 'Price');
            const priceText = autoGetPriceText(action);
            let subject = '';
            if (!name) {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} a car`;
            } else if (action === 'Trading' && trade) {
                return `Trading ${name} for ${q(trade)}.`;
            } else if (action === 'Selling or trading' && trade) {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${name} for ${q(trade)}`;
            } else {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${name}`;
            }
            if (name.toLowerCase().includes('monowheel') && monoVal) {
                subject += ` of type ${monoVal}`;
            }
            let sentence = subject;
            sentence += autoJoin(feats);
            if (!sentence.endsWith('.')) sentence += '.';
            return action === 'Trading' ? sentence : `${sentence} ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function autoUpdate() {
            autoOutput.textContent = autoBuild();
        }
        autoCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(autoOutput.textContent.trim());
                autoCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => autoCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });

            autoPriceSelect.value = '';
            autoFeatures.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            setActiveAuto(autoActionGroup, autoActionGroup.children[0]);
            autoUpdate();
        });
        autoUpdate();

        // ================= Case container Formatter =================
        const caseAction = document.getElementById('caseAction');
        const caseName = document.getElementById('caseName');
        const caseCount = document.getElementById('caseCount');

        function setActiveCase(container, el) {
            [...container.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            caseUpdate();
        }

        function getActiveCase(container) {
            const a = container && container.querySelector('.active');
            return a ? a.dataset.value : '';
        }

        function titleCaseCaseName(v) {
            if (!v) return '';
            return v.split(/\s+/).map(w => w ? (w[0].toUpperCase() + w.slice(1)) : '').join(' ');
        }

        function buildCaseSentence() {
            const action = getActiveCase(caseAction) || 'Selling';
            const raw = (caseName && caseName.value || '').trim();
            const name = raw || 'Progen container';
            const pluralBase = name.endsWith('container') ? (name.replace(/container$/, 'containers')) : (name + 's');
            const countRaw = (caseCount && caseCount.value || '').trim();
            const count = countRaw && /^\d+$/.test(countRaw) ? parseInt(countRaw, 10) : undefined;

            // Get all currently active mode chips
            const activeChips = caseModeGroup ? [...caseModeGroup.querySelectorAll('.chip.active')] : [];
            // Get modes from active chips
            const modes = activeChips.map(chip => chip.dataset.mode);
            const priceRaw = casePriceCustom ? casePriceCustom.value.trim() : '';
            const priceText = parseCustomPrice(priceRaw) || '';
            const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

            // Check which modes are selected
            const hasEach = modes.includes('each');
            const hasBulk = modes.includes('bulk');

            // Build sentence according to rules
            if (!count || count === 1) {
                // singular
                const pricePart = punctuatePrice(priceText || 'Negotiable');
                // For Negotiable, keep the period; for other prices, remove it
                const cleanPrice = (priceText || 'Negotiable') === 'Negotiable' ? pricePart : (pricePart.endsWith('.') ? pricePart.slice(0, -1) : pricePart);

                // If bulk is selected, show bulk format
                if (hasBulk) {
                    // If both bulk and each are selected, show "each" after price
                    if (hasEach && priceText) {
                        return `${capitalize(action)} ${pluralBase} in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${cleanPrice} each.`;
                    }
                    return `${capitalize(action)} ${pluralBase} in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${cleanPrice}`;
                }

                // If only "each" is selected or no mode is selected (default to "each"), show "each" after price
                if (hasEach && priceText) {
                    return `${capitalize(action)} ${pluralBase}. ${priceWord}: ${cleanPrice} each.`;
                }

                return `${capitalize(action)} ${pluralBase}. ${priceWord}: ${cleanPrice}`;
            }

            // For count > 1
            const pricePart = punctuatePrice(priceText || 'Negotiable');
            // For Negotiable, keep the period; for other prices, remove it
            const cleanPrice = (priceText || 'Negotiable') === 'Negotiable' ? pricePart : (pricePart.endsWith('.') ? pricePart.slice(0, -1) : pricePart);

            // Handle different mode combinations
            if (hasBulk) {
                // If both bulk and each are selected, show "each" after price
                if (hasEach && priceText) {
                    return `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${pluralBase} in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${cleanPrice} each.`;
                }
                return `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${pluralBase} in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${cleanPrice}`;
            } else if (hasEach && priceText) {
                return `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${pluralBase}. ${priceWord}: ${cleanPrice} each.`;
            } else {
                return `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${pluralBase}. ${priceWord}: ${cleanPrice}`;
            }
        }

        function caseUpdate() {
            if (caseOutput) caseOutput.textContent = buildCaseSentence();
            const a = getActiveCase(caseAction) || 'Selling';
            if (casePriceLabel) casePriceLabel.textContent = (a === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price');
        }
        caseAction && caseAction.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');

            setActiveCase(caseAction, el);
        });

        caseCount && caseCount.addEventListener('input', caseUpdate);

            const el = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!el) return;
            el.classList.toggle('active');
            caseUpdate();
        });
        casePriceCustom && casePriceCustom.addEventListener('input', caseUpdate);
        bindMillionQuickSet(casePriceMillionNum, casePriceMillionBtn);
        caseCopyBtn && caseCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((caseOutput && caseOutput.textContent || '').trim());
                caseCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => caseCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        caseClearBtn && caseClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (caseName) caseName.value = '';
            if (caseCount) caseCount.value = '';
            if (casePriceCustom) casePriceCustom.value = '';
            // Reset mode chips - clear all selections and set 'each' as active
            if (caseModeGroup) {
                [...caseModeGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                caseModeGroup.querySelector('[data-mode="each"]').classList.add('active');
            }
            if (caseAction) setActiveCase(caseAction, caseAction.children[0]);
            caseUpdate();
        });

        caseUpdate();

        // ================= Business Section Formatter =================
        const bizActionGroup = document.getElementById('bizActionGroup');

        const bizName = document.getElementById('bizName');
        const bizNumber = document.getElementById('bizNumber');
        const bizBeds = document.getElementById('bizBeds');

        const bizPlantationCategoryField = document.getElementById('bizPlantationCategoryField');

        const bizInSelect = document.getElementById('bizInSelect');
        const bizInCustom = document.getElementById('bizInCustom');
        const bizNearSelect = document.getElementById('bizNearSelect');
        const bizNearCustom = document.getElementById('bizNearCustom');
        const bizPriceLabel = document.getElementById('bizPriceLabel');
        const bizPriceSelect = document.getElementById('bizPriceSelect');
        const bizPriceCustom = document.getElementById('bizPriceCustom');
        const bizPriceMillionNum = document.getElementById('bizPriceMillionNum');
        const bizPriceMillionBtn = document.getElementById('bizPriceMillionBtn');
        const bizOutput = document.getElementById('bizOutput');
        const bizCopyBtn = document.getElementById('bizCopyBtn');
        const bizClearBtn = document.getElementById('bizClearBtn');

        function setActiveBiz(container, el) {
            [...container.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            bizUpdate();
        }

        function getActiveBiz(container) {
            const a = container ? container.querySelector('.active') : null;
            return a ? a.dataset.value : '';
        }

        bizActionGroup && bizActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActiveBiz(bizActionGroup, el);

            if (a === 'Buying') bizPriceLabel.textContent = 'Budget';
            else bizPriceLabel.textContent = 'Price';

            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;

            togglePlantationFields();
        });

        [bizName, bizNumber, bizPriceCustom].forEach(el => el && el.addEventListener('input', bizUpdate));
