        bizBeds && bizBeds.addEventListener('change', bizUpdate);
        bizName && bizName.addEventListener('input', () => {
            togglePlantationFields();
            bizUpdate();
        });
        bizPriceSelect && bizPriceSelect.addEventListener('change', bizUpdate);;
        [bizInSelect, bizNearSelect].forEach(sel => sel && sel.addEventListener('change', () => {
            const isCustom = sel.value === '__custom';
            const box = sel === bizInSelect ? bizInCustom : bizNearCustom;
            if (box) box.style.display = isCustom ? '' : 'none';
            bizUpdate();
        }));
        bindMillionQuickSet(bizPriceMillionNum, bizPriceMillionBtn);;
        [bizInCustom, bizNearCustom].forEach(el => el && el.addEventListener('input', bizUpdate));

        function bizTitleCase(value) {
            if (!value) return '';
            return value.split(/\s+/).map(w => w ? w[0].toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase() : '').join(' ');
        }

        function bizGetLocationValue(selectEl, customEl) {
            if (!selectEl) return '';
            if (selectEl.value === '__custom') return bizTitleCase((customEl && customEl.value || '').trim());
            return bizTitleCase((selectEl.value || '').trim());
        }

        function normalizeBizName(raw) {
            let s = (raw || '').trim();
            if (!s) return '';
            const lower = s.toLowerCase();
            // Basic replacements per rules
            let out = lower
                .replace(/personal\s+business/g, 'private business')
                .replace(/\bdrug\s*lab\b/g, 'burger shop')
                .replace(/burger\s*shop\s*drug\s*lab/g, 'burger shop')
                .replace(/\bauto\s*workshop\s*(?:biz|business)?\b/g, 'service station')
                .replace(/\bworkshop\b/g, 'service station')
                .replace(/\bgun\s*store\b|\bweapon\s*store\b/g, 'ammunition store')
                .replace(/\bstrip\s*club\b/g, 'bar')
                .replace(/\bcharging\s*station\b/g, 'electric station')
                .replace(/\bbarber\b/g, 'hair salon')
                .replace(/\bbinco\b|\bsuburban\b/g, 'clothing shop')
                .replace(/\b24\s*\/\s*7\b/g, '24/7')
                .replace(/\batm\b/g, 'atm');
            // Collapse multiple spaces
            out = out.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();

            // Return business name as originally added (preserve capitalization)
            return s;
        }

        function bizGetPriceText(action) {
            // If custom is filled, it overrides presets
            const vCustom = (bizPriceCustom && bizPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            if (vCustom) {
                return parseCustomPrice(vCustom);
            }
            // Otherwise use preset value or Negotiable
            if (bizPriceSelect && bizPriceSelect.value) {
                return bizPriceSelect.value;
            }
            return 'Negotiable';
        }

        function isPlantationName(name) {
            return /\bplantation\b/i.test(name || '');
        }

        function togglePlantationFields() {
            const kind = getActiveBiz(bizKindGroup) || 'Business';
            const plantation = kind === 'Plantation';
            if (bizPlantationCategoryField) bizPlantationCategoryField.style.display = plantation ? '' : 'none';
            if (bizBedsField) bizBedsField.style.display = plantation ? '' : 'none';
        }

        function buildBizSentence() {
            const action = getActiveBiz(bizActionGroup) || 'Selling';
            const kind = getActiveBiz(bizKindGroup) || 'Business';
            let name = normalizeBizName(bizName && bizName.value);
            const numberRaw = (bizNumber && bizNumber.value || '').trim();
            const bedsRaw = (bizBeds && bizBeds.value || '').trim();
            const inLoc = bizGetLocationValue(bizInSelect, bizInCustom);
            const nearLoc = bizGetLocationValue(bizNearSelect, bizNearCustom);
            const priceText = bizGetPriceText(action);
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

            // Enforce allowed actions (Buying/Selling/Selling or trading)
            // If somehow different, default to Selling
            const allowed = new Set(['Selling', 'Buying', 'Selling or trading']);
            const finalAction = allowed.has(action) ? action : 'Selling';

            // If client doesn’t specify the number, we add the word "business" after name (unless name already ends with business/control/etc.)
            const hasNumber = numberRaw !== '';
            if (!name) {
                name = 'private business';
            }

            // Plantation special handling
            const plantationCategory = (bizPlantationCategory && bizPlantationCategory.value) || '';
            // Default needsBusinessWord based on whether name ends with business/control and number presence
            const lowerName = name.toLowerCase();
            let needsBusinessWord = !hasNumber && !/(business|control)$/i.test(lowerName);

            if (kind === 'Plantation') {
                // Prefer explicit plantation category if provided, otherwise generic 'Plantation'
                if (plantationCategory) {
                    name = `${capitalize(plantationCategory)} plantation`;
                } else {
                    name = 'Plantation';
                }
                // For plantations we always want the word "business" appended in display (per examples)
                needsBusinessWord = true;
            }
            const displayName = needsBusinessWord ? `${name} business` : name;

            let subject = '';
            if (hasNumber) {
                // With number: "Selling ATM №1"
                // Special case: if name is "private business", use article "a" and keep lowercase
                if (name && name.toLowerCase() === 'private business') {
                    subject = `${capitalize(finalAction)} a private business №${numberRaw}`;
                } else {
                    subject = `${capitalize(finalAction)} ${capitalize(name)} №${numberRaw}`;
                }
            } else if (kind === 'Plantation') {
                // Plantation formats:
                // - "Selling Cabbage plantation with 10 beds"
                // - "Buying Cabbage plantation business with 10 beds"
                const displayName = needsBusinessWord ? `${capitalize(name)} business` : capitalize(name);
                subject = `${capitalize(finalAction)} ${displayName}`;
            } else {
                // Regular business formats:
                // - "Selling ATM business"
                // - "Buying Gas Station business"
                // - "Selling a private business" (when name is default "private business")
                const displayName = needsBusinessWord ? `${capitalize(name)} business` : capitalize(name);
                // Special case: if name is "private business", use article "a" and keep lowercase
                if (name && name.toLowerCase() === 'private business' && !hasNumber) {
                    subject = `${capitalize(finalAction)} a private business`;
                } else {
                    subject = `${capitalize(finalAction)} ${displayName}`;
                }
            }

            const details = [];
            // Handle plantation beds
            if (kind === 'Plantation' && bedsRaw) {
                details.push(`with ${bedsRaw} beds`);
            }

            // Build main sentence
            let sentence = subject;
            if (details.length) {
                sentence += ' ' + details.join(', ');
            }
            if (!sentence.endsWith('.')) sentence += '.';

            // Add location if specified
            if (inLoc) {
                sentence = sentence.slice(0, -1) + ` in ${inLoc}.`;
            }
            if (nearLoc) {
                sentence = sentence.slice(0, -1) + ` near ${nearLoc}.`;
            }

            return `${sentence} ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/\s\./g, '.');
        }

        function bizUpdate() {
            try {
                if (bizOutput) {
                    const sentence = buildBizSentence();
                    bizOutput.textContent = sentence;
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Error in bizUpdate:', e);
                if (bizOutput) bizOutput.textContent = 'Error: ' + e.message;
            }
        }

        // ================= Dating Section Formatter =================
        const datingChips = document.getElementById('datingChips');
        const datingPersonField = document.getElementById('datingPersonField');
        const datingPerson = document.getElementById('datingPerson');
        const datingOutput = document.getElementById('datingOutput');
        const datingCopyBtn = document.getElementById('datingCopyBtn');
        const datingClearBtn = document.getElementById('datingClearBtn');

        function normalizePersonName(raw) {
            const s = (raw || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
            if (!s) return '';
            let parts = s.split(' ');
            // If already two or more words, take the first two
            if (parts.length >= 2) {
                parts = [parts[0], parts[1]];
            } else {
                // Single token -> try to split into First + Last
                const token = parts[0].toLowerCase();
                let first = '';
                let last = '';
                // 1) Try suffix-based split for common last names
                const commonSuffixes = ['pandit', 'patel', 'sharma', 'kumar', 'gupta', 'singh', 'khan', 'ali', 'ahmed', 'joshi', 'verma', 'kapoor', 'mehta', 'rao', 'nair', 'iyer', 'das'];
                const matchSuffix = commonSuffixes.find(suf => token.endsWith(suf) && token.length > suf.length + 1);
                if (matchSuffix) {
                    first = token.slice(0, token.length - matchSuffix.length);
                    last = matchSuffix;
                }
                // 2) Try camelCase boundary (lower->upper)
                if (!first) {
                    const idx = (raw || '').search(/[a-z][A-Z]/);
                    if (idx > 0) {
                        first = token.slice(0, idx + 1);
                        last = token.slice(idx + 1);
                    }
                }
                // 3) Heuristic split near the middle on vowel boundary
                if (!first) {
                    const mid = Math.floor(token.length / 2);
                    const isVowel = (ch) => /[aeiou]/.test(ch);
                    let cut = -1;
                    for (let i = mid; i >= 2; i--) {
                        if (isVowel(token[i - 1]) && !isVowel(token[i])) {
                            cut = i;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (cut === -1) {
                        for (let i = mid; i < token.length - 2; i++) {
                            if (isVowel(token[i]) && !isVowel(token[i + 1])) {
                                cut = i + 1;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (cut === -1) {
                        cut = Math.max(2, Math.min(token.length - 2, mid));
                    }
                    first = token.slice(0, cut);
                    last = token.slice(cut);
                }
                parts = [first, last];
            }
            const norm = parts.map(w => w ? w[0].toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase() : '').join(' ');
            return norm;
        }

        function datingBuild() {
            const person = normalizePersonName(datingPerson && datingPerson.value);
            if (person) {
                return `Looking for ${person}.`;
            }
            return 'Looking for First Last.';
        }

        function datingUpdate() {
            if (datingOutput) datingOutput.textContent = datingBuild();
        }

        // ================= Work Section Formatter =================
        const workActionGroup = document.getElementById('workActionGroup');
        const workRole = document.getElementById('workRole');
        const workSiteNo = document.getElementById('workSiteNo');
        const workPlace = document.getElementById('workPlace');
        const workYears = document.getElementById('workYears');
        const workPriceSelect = document.getElementById('workPriceSelect');
        const workPriceCustom = document.getElementById('workPriceCustom');
        const workPriceMillionNum = document.getElementById('workPriceMillionNum');

        function setActiveWork(container, el) {

        function formatWorkMoney(text) {
            // Reuse pricing normalization from auto/real estate
            return parseCustomPrice(text);
        }

        function getWorkPrice() {
            const c = (workPriceCustom && workPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            if (c) {
                return formatWorkMoney(c);
            }
            if (workPriceSelect && workPriceSelect.value) return workPriceSelect.value;
            return 'Negotiable';
        }

        function workSiteLocation(no) {
            if (no === '1') return 'in Vespucci Boulevard';
            if (no === '2') return 'in Calais Avenue';
            if (no === '3') return 'in Pillbox Hill';
            return '';
        }

        function normalizeRole(raw) {
            const r = (raw || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            if (!r) return '';
            if (r === 'stripper' || r === 'strippers') return 'professional dancer';
            return r;
        }

        function pluralizeRole(r) {
            if (/s$/.test(r)) return r;
            if (r === 'dj') return 'DJs';
            if (r === 'lawyer') return 'Lawyers';
            if (r === 'trucker') return 'Truckers';
            if (r === 'bodyguard') return 'Bodyguards';
            if (r === 'photographer') return 'Photographers';
            if (r === 'assistant') return 'Assistants';
            if (r === 'professional dancer') return 'professional dancers';
            if (r === 'personal driver') return 'Personal drivers';
            if (r === 'professional singer') return 'Professional Singers';
            if (r === 'firefighters') return 'Firefighters';
            return r[0].toUpperCase() + r.slice(1) + (r.endsWith('er') ? 's' : '');
        }

        function titleCaseRole(r) {
            if (!r) return '';
            if (r === 'dj') return 'DJ';
            return r.split(/\s+/).map(w => w ? w[0].toUpperCase() + w.slice(1) : '').join(' ');
        }

        function workBuild() {
            const action = getActiveWork(workActionGroup) || 'Hiring';
            let role = normalizeRole(workRole && workRole.value);
            const siteNo = (workSiteNo && workSiteNo.value) || '';
            const place = (workPlace && workPlace.value || '').trim();
            const years = (workYears && workYears.value || '').trim();
            const price = getWorkPrice();
            const payMode = getActiveWork(workPayMode) || 'Salary';
            const isRenting = (action === 'Renting' || action === 'Renting out');

            // role handling
            const isConstruction = role === 'construction' || role === 'workers';
            let subject = '';
            if (action === 'Looking for a job') {
                if (isConstruction) {
                    subject = 'Looking for a job at the construction site';
                } else if (role === 'solar panel plantation') {
                    subject = 'Looking for solar panel plantation work';
                } else if (role) {
                    subject = `Looking to work as a ${titleCaseRole(role)}`;
                } else {
                    subject = 'Looking for a job';
                }
            } else if (action === 'Looking to hire') {
                subject = 'Looking to hire';
                if (role && !isConstruction) subject += ` a ${titleCaseRole(role)}`;
            } else if (isRenting) {
                // Rent ads examples
                subject = 'Renting';
                if (action === 'Renting out') subject = 'Renting out';
                if (role) {
                    subject += ` ${role}`;
                }
            } else { // Hiring
                if (isConstruction) {
                    subject = 'Hiring workers at construction site';
                } else if (role === 'solar panel plantation') {
                    subject = 'Hiring workers for solar panel plantations';
                } else if (role === 'professional dancer') {
                    subject = 'Hiring professional dancers';
                } else if (role) {
                    subject = `Hiring ${role === 'firefighters' ? 'firefighters' : (role === 'assistant' ? 'an assistant' : (role.match(/^[aeiou]/) ? 'an ' : 'a ') + titleCaseRole(role))}`;
                } else {
                    subject = 'Hiring';
                }
            }

            // construction site number and mapped location
            const siteBits = [];
            if (isConstruction) {
                if (siteNo) {
                    siteBits.push(`№${siteNo}`);
                    const mapped = workSiteLocation(siteNo);
                    if (mapped) siteBits.push(mapped);
                }
            }
            let sentence = subject;
            if (isConstruction && siteBits.length) {
                sentence += ' ' + siteBits[0];
                if (siteBits[1]) sentence += ', ' + siteBits[1];
            }
            if (!isConstruction && place) {
                sentence += ` at ${place}`;
            }
            if (years) {
                sentence += ` with ${years} years of experience`;
            }
            if (!sentence.endsWith('.')) sentence += '.';

            // pricing & perks
            let priceLine = '';
            if (isRenting) {
                // Renting uses Rent and may add per week
                const rentWord = action === 'Renting out' ? 'Budget' : 'Rent';
                const perWeek = (workPerWeek && workPerWeek.classList.contains('active'));
                if (perWeek) {
                    priceLine = `${rentWord}: ${price} per week.`;
                } else {
                    priceLine = `${rentWord}: ${punctuatePrice(price)}`;
                }
            } else if (action === 'Looking for a job') {
                priceLine = `Salary: ${punctuatePrice('Negotiable')}`;
            } else {
                if (/^\$/.test(price) && payMode === 'Awarding bonus') priceLine = `Awarding ${punctuatePrice(price)} bonus`;
                else priceLine = `${payMode}: ${punctuatePrice(price)}`;
            }

            // perks only when no specific amount mentioned
            const perksActive = [...(workPerks ? workPerks.querySelectorAll('.chip.active') : [])].map(c => c.dataset.key);
            const hasSpecificMoney = /^\$/.test(price);
            if (perksActive.length && !hasSpecificMoney) {
                const perkTexts = [];
                if (perksActive.includes('awardingBonuses')) perkTexts.push('Awarding bonuses');
                if (perksActive.includes('payingWell')) perkTexts.push('Paying well');
                if (perkTexts.length) {
                    sentence = sentence.replace(/\.$/, '');
                    sentence += '. ' + perkTexts.join(' and ') + '.';
                }
            }

            return `${sentence} ${priceLine}`.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/\s\./g, '.');
        }

        function workUpdate() {
            if (workOutput) workOutput.textContent = workBuild();
        }
        workCopyBtn && workCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((workOutput && workOutput.textContent || '').trim());
                workCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => workCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        workClearBtn && workClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            ;
            [workRole, workSiteNo, workPlace, workYears, workPriceCustom].forEach(el => el && (el.value = ''));
            if (workPriceSelect) workPriceSelect.value = '';
            if (workActionGroup) setActiveWork(workActionGroup, workActionGroup.children[0]);
            if (workPayMode) setActiveWork(workPayMode, workPayMode.children[0]);
            workPerks && workPerks.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            workRentOpts && (workRentOpts.style.display = 'none');
            workUpdate();
        });
        workUpdate();

        function buildPetSentence() {
            const action = getActivePet(petActionGroup) || 'Selling';
            const nameRaw = (petName && petName.value || '').trim();
            const hasName = !!nameRaw;
            const properName = nameRaw;
            const article = hasName ? (startsWithVowel(properName) ? 'an ' : 'a ') : 'a ';
            const subject = hasName ? `${capitalize(action)} cage with ${article}${properName}` : `${capitalize(action)} cage with a pet`;
            const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const customRaw = (petPriceCustom && petPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

            if (petOutput) petOutput.textContent = buildPetSentence();
            const a = getActivePet(petActionGroup) || 'Selling';
            if (petPriceLabel) petPriceLabel.textContent = (a === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price');
        }

            if (actionEl) setActivePet(petActionGroup, actionEl);
            // Do not set a custom price here; user can type or use Million quick-set

            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);
                chip.classList.add('active');
                setTimeout(() => chip.classList.remove('active'), 400);

                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        petCopyBtn && petCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {

        // Backpack logic
        const backpackItem = document.getElementById('backpackItem');
        const backpackAction = document.getElementById('backpackAction');
        const backpackQuality = document.getElementById('backpackQuality');
        const backpackQualityContainer = document.getElementById('backpackQualityContainer');
        const backpackCount = document.getElementById('backpackCount');
        const backpackSkinName = document.getElementById('backpackSkinName');
        const backpackSkinField = document.getElementById('backpackSkinField');
        const backpackOutput = document.getElementById('backpackOutput');
        const backpackCopyBtn = document.getElementById('backpackCopyBtn');
        const backpackClearBtn = document.getElementById('backpackClearBtn');

        function setActiveBackpack(container, el) {
            [...container.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            backpackUpdate();
        }

        function getActiveBackpack(container) {
            const a = container && container.querySelector('.active');
            return a ? a.dataset.value : '';
        }

        function buildBackpackSentence() {
            const item = (getActiveBackpack(backpackItem) || 'inventory').toLowerCase();
            const action = getActiveBackpack(backpackAction) || 'Selling';
            const q = (getActiveBackpack(backpackQuality) || 'high').toLowerCase();
            const countRaw = (backpackCount && backpackCount.value || '').trim();
            const count = countRaw && /^\d+$/.test(countRaw) ? parseInt(countRaw, 10) : undefined;
            const skinName = (backpackSkinName && backpackSkinName.value || '').trim();
            const qualityWord = q === 'max' ? 'max' : q; // keep as given
            let subject = '';
            if (item === 'backpack skin') {
                if (skinName) {
                    // Use specific backpack skin name
                    if (count && count > 1) {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${skinName}s`;
                    } else {
                        const article = (action === 'Buying' || action === 'Selling') ? (startsWithVowel(skinName) ? 'an ' : 'a ') : '';
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${article}${skinName}`;
                    }
                } else {
                    // Generic backpack skin
                    if (count && count > 1) {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} backpack skins`;
                    } else {
                        const article = (action === 'Buying' || action === 'Selling') ? (startsWithVowel('backpack') ? 'an ' : 'a ') : '';
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${article}backpack skin`;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                if (count && count > 1) {
                    subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${qualityWord} quality inventory`;
                } else {
                    subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${qualityWord} quality inventory`;
                }
            }
            const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const customRaw = (backpackPriceCustom && backpackPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function backpackUpdate() {
            if (backpackOutput) backpackOutput.textContent = buildBackpackSentence();
        }

        function toggleBackpackQuality() {
            const item = getActiveBackpack(backpackItem) || 'inventory';
            if (backpackQualityContainer) backpackQualityContainer.style.display = (item === 'inventory') ? '' : 'none';
        }

        function toggleBackpackSkinField() {
            const item = getActiveBackpack(backpackItem) || 'inventory';
            if (backpackSkinField) backpackSkinField.style.display = (item === 'backpack skin') ? '' : 'none';
        }
        backpackItem && backpackItem.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActiveBackpack(backpackItem, el);
            toggleBackpackQuality();
            toggleBackpackSkinField();
        });
        backpackAction && backpackAction.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActiveBackpack(backpackAction, el);
        });
        backpackQuality && backpackQuality.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActiveBackpack(backpackQuality, el);
        });
        backpackCount && backpackCount.addEventListener('input', backpackUpdate);
        backpackSkinName && backpackSkinName.addEventListener('input', backpackUpdate);
        const backpackPriceCustom = document.getElementById('backpackPriceCustom');
        const backpackPriceMillionNum = document.getElementById('backpackPriceMillionNum');
        const backpackPriceMillionBtn = document.getElementById('backpackPriceMillionBtn');
        backpackPriceCustom && backpackPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', backpackUpdate);
        bindMillionQuickSet(backpackPriceMillionNum, backpackPriceMillionBtn);
        backpackCopyBtn && backpackCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((backpackOutput && backpackOutput.textContent || '').trim());
                backpackCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => backpackCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        backpackClearBtn && backpackClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (backpackCount) backpackCount.value = '';
            if (backpackPriceCustom) backpackPriceCustom.value = '';
            if (backpackSkinName) backpackSkinName.value = '';
            if (backpackItem) setActiveBackpack(backpackItem, backpackItem.children[0]);
            if (backpackAction) setActiveBackpack(backpackAction, backpackAction.children[0]);
            if (backpackQuality) setActiveBackpack(backpackQuality, backpackQuality.children[2]);
            toggleBackpackQuality();
            toggleBackpackSkinField();
            backpackUpdate();
        });
        toggleBackpackQuality();
        toggleBackpackSkinField();
        backpackUpdate();

        // ================= Pickaxe Section Formatter =================
        const pickaxeAction = document.getElementById('pickaxeAction');
        const pickaxeQuality = document.getElementById('pickaxeQuality');
        const pickaxeCount = document.getElementById('pickaxeCount');
        const pickaxePriceCustom = document.getElementById('pickaxePriceCustom');
        const pickaxePriceMillionNum = document.getElementById('pickaxePriceMillionNum');
        const pickaxePriceMillionBtn = document.getElementById('pickaxePriceMillionBtn');
        const pickaxeOutput = document.getElementById('pickaxeOutput');
        const pickaxeCopyBtn = document.getElementById('pickaxeCopyBtn');
        const pickaxeClearBtn = document.getElementById('pickaxeClearBtn');

        function setActivePickaxe(container, el) {
            [...container.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            pickaxeUpdate();
        }

        function getActivePickaxe(container) {
            const a = container && container.querySelector('.active');
            return a ? a.dataset.value : '';
        }

        function buildPickaxeSentence() {
            const action = getActivePickaxe(pickaxeAction) || 'Selling';
            const q = (getActivePickaxe(pickaxeQuality) || 'high').toLowerCase();
            const countRaw = (pickaxeCount && pickaxeCount.value || '').toString().trim();
            const count = countRaw === '' ? undefined : (Number.isFinite(Number(countRaw)) ? Math.max(1, parseInt(countRaw, 10)) : undefined);
            const qualityWord = q === 'max' ? 'max' : q;
            let subject = '';
            if (count && count > 1) {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${qualityWord} quality pickaxes`;
            } else {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${qualityWord} quality pickaxe`;
            }
            const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const customRaw = (pickaxePriceCustom && pickaxePriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function pickaxeUpdate() {
            if (pickaxeOutput) pickaxeOutput.textContent = buildPickaxeSentence();
            const a = getActivePickaxe(pickaxeAction) || 'Selling';
            const label = (a === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            if (document.getElementById('pickaxePriceLabel')) document.getElementById('pickaxePriceLabel').textContent = label;
        }

        pickaxeAction && pickaxeAction.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActivePickaxe(pickaxeAction, el);
        });
        pickaxeQuality && pickaxeQuality.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActivePickaxe(pickaxeQuality, el);
        });
        pickaxeCount && pickaxeCount.addEventListener('input', pickaxeUpdate);
        pickaxePriceCustom && pickaxePriceCustom.addEventListener('input', pickaxeUpdate);
        bindMillionQuickSet(pickaxePriceMillionNum, pickaxePriceMillionBtn);

        pickaxeCopyBtn && pickaxeCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((pickaxeOutput && pickaxeOutput.textContent || '').trim());
                pickaxeCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => pickaxeCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        pickaxeClearBtn && pickaxeClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (pickaxeCount) pickaxeCount.value = '';
            if (pickaxePriceCustom) pickaxePriceCustom.value = '';
            if (pickaxeAction) setActivePickaxe(pickaxeAction, pickaxeAction.children[0]);
            if (pickaxeQuality) setActivePickaxe(pickaxeQuality, pickaxeQuality.children[2]);
            pickaxeUpdate();
        });
        pickaxeUpdate();

        // ================= Fishing rod Section Formatter =================
        const fishingAction = document.getElementById('fishingActionGroup');
        const fishingQuality = document.getElementById('fishingQuality');
        const fishingCount = document.getElementById('fishingCount');
        const fishingPriceCustom = document.getElementById('fishingPriceCustom');
        const fishingOutput = document.getElementById('fishingOutput');
        const fishingCopyBtn = document.getElementById('fishingCopyBtn');
        const fishingClearBtn = document.getElementById('fishingClearBtn');

        function setActiveFishing(container, el) {
            [...container.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            fishingUpdate();
        }

        function getActiveFishing(container) {
            const a = container && container.querySelector('.active');
            return a ? a.dataset.value : '';
        }

        function buildFishingSentence() {
            const action = getActiveFishing(fishingAction) || 'Selling';
            const q = (getActiveFishing(fishingQuality) || 'high').toLowerCase();
            const countRaw = (fishingCount && fishingCount.value || '').toString().trim();
            const count = countRaw === '' ? undefined : (Number.isFinite(Number(countRaw)) ? Math.max(1, parseInt(countRaw, 10)) : undefined);
            const qualityWord = q;
            let subject = '';
            if (count && count > 1) {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${qualityWord} quality fishing rods`;
            } else {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${qualityWord} quality fishing rod`;
            }
            const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const customRaw = (fishingPriceCustom && fishingPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function fishingUpdate() {
            if (fishingOutput) fishingOutput.textContent = buildFishingSentence();
            const a = getActiveFishing(fishingAction) || 'Selling';
            const label = (a === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            if (document.getElementById('fishingPriceLabel')) document.getElementById('fishingPriceLabel').textContent = label;
        }

            setActiveFishing(fishingAction, el);

            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActiveFishing(fishingQuality, el);
        });
        fishingCount && fishingCount.addEventListener('input', fishingUpdate);
        fishingPriceCustom && fishingPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', fishingUpdate);
        fishingCopyBtn && fishingCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {

                fishingCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => fishingCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        fishingClearBtn && fishingClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (fishingCount) fishingCount.value = '';
            if (fishingPriceCustom) fishingPriceCustom.value = '';
            if (fishingAction) setActiveFishing(fishingAction, fishingAction.children[0]);
            if (fishingQuality) setActiveFishing(fishingQuality, fishingQuality.children[2]);
            fishingUpdate();

        // ================= Christmas Resources Section Formatter =================
        const christmasActionGroup = document.getElementById('christmasActionGroup');
        const christmasItemsChips = document.getElementById('christmasItemsChips');
        const christmasCount = document.getElementById('christmasCount');
        const christmasPriceCustom = document.getElementById('christmasPriceCustom');
        const christmasOutput = document.getElementById('christmasOutput');
        const christmasCopyBtn = document.getElementById('christmasCopyBtn');
        const christmasClearBtn = document.getElementById('christmasClearBtn');
        const christmasEachBtn = document.getElementById('christmasEachBtn');
        const christmasBulkBtn = document.getElementById('christmasBulkBtn');

        let selectedChristmasItem = null;
        let selectedFormat = null; // Track if 'each' or 'bulk' is selected

        function buildChristmasAd() {
            const action = (christmasActionGroup && christmasActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            const item = selectedChristmasItem || 'lollipop';
            const countRaw = (christmasCount && christmasCount.value || '').toString().trim();
            const count = countRaw && /^\d+$/.test(countRaw) ? parseInt(countRaw, 10) : undefined;
            const customRaw = (christmasPriceCustom && christmasPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

            // Get selected formats
            const formatChips = christmasFormat && christmasFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip.active') || [];
            const formats = Array.from(formatChips).map(chip => chip.dataset.format);
            const isEach = formats.includes('each');
            const isBulk = formats.includes('bulk');

            let subject = '';

            // Handle special pluralization rules
            if (isBulk) {
                // Special handling for bulk format - no articles and use appropriate form
                if (count && count > 1) {
                    // For 'lollipop', 'gift' and 'drawing' we add 's' for plural
                    if (item === 'lollipop' || item === 'newyearsgift' || item === 'littlegift' || item === 'biggift' || item === 'drawing') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item === 'lollipop' ? 'Christmas lollipops' : item === 'newyearsgift' ? 'New years gifts' : item === 'littlegift' ? 'Little gifts' : item === 'biggift' ? 'Big gifts' : 'Drawings'}`;
                    } else {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item}s`;
                    }
                } else {
                    // For bulk with single item, use plural form without article
                    if (item === 'lollipop') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} Christmas lollipops`;
                    } else if (item === 'newyearsgift') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} New years gifts`;
                    } else if (item === 'littlegift') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} Little gifts`;
                    } else if (item === 'biggift') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} Big gifts`;
                    } else if (item === 'drawing') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} Drawings`;
                    } else {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${item}s`;
                    }
                }
                subject += ' in bulk';
            } else {
                // Normal handling (each format or no format)
                if (count && count > 1) {
                    // For 'lollipop', 'gift' and 'drawing' we add 's' for plural
                    if (item === 'lollipop' || item === 'newyearsgift' || item === 'littlegift' || item === 'biggift' || item === 'drawing') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item === 'lollipop' ? 'Christmas lollipops' : item === 'newyearsgift' ? 'New years gifts' : item === 'littlegift' ? 'Little gifts' : item === 'biggift' ? 'Big gifts' : 'Drawings'}`;
                    } else {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item}s`;
                    }
                } else if (count == 1) {
                    // If count is 1, treat as singular regardless of the number
                    if (item === 'lollipop') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} a Christmas lollipop`;
                    } else if (item === 'newyearsgift') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} a New years gift`;
                    } else if (item === 'littlegift') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} a Little gift`;
                    } else if (item === 'biggift') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} a Big gift`;
                    } else {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} a ${item}`;
                    }
                } else {
                    // No count specified
                    if (item === 'lollipop') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} a Christmas lollipop`;
                    } else if (item === 'newyearsgift') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} a New years gift`;
                    } else if (item === 'littlegift') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} a Little gift`;
                    } else if (item === 'biggift') {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} a Big gift`;
                    } else {
                        // Use 'a' or 'an' based on first letter
                        const article = (item[0].toLowerCase() === 'a' || item[0].toLowerCase() === 'e' ||
                            item[0].toLowerCase() === 'i' || item[0].toLowerCase() === 'o' ||
                            item[0].toLowerCase() === 'u') ? 'an' : 'a';
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${article} ${item}`;
                    }
                }
            }

            // Handle pricing format
            if (isEach && priceText !== 'Negotiable') {
                return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${priceText} each.`;
            } else {
                return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            }
        }

        function christmasUpdate() {
            if (christmasOutput) christmasOutput.textContent = buildChristmasAd();
            // Update price label based on action
            const action = christmasActionGroup && christmasActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
            const label = document.getElementById('christmasPriceLabel');
            if (label) label.textContent = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
        }

        // Handle action radio buttons
        christmasActionGroup && christmasActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            [...christmasActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            christmasUpdate();
        });

        // Handle Christmas items chips
        christmasItemsChips && christmasItemsChips.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            [...christmasItemsChips.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            chip.classList.add('active');
            selectedChristmasItem = chip.dataset.item;
            christmasUpdate();
        });

        // Handle count and price changes
        [christmasCount, christmasPriceCustom].forEach(el => {
            el && el.addEventListener('input', christmasUpdate);
        });

        // Format toggle group (like Resource Mining)
        christmasFormat && christmasFormat.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            chip.classList.toggle('active');
            christmasUpdate();
        });

        // Copy button
        christmasCopyBtn && christmasCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((christmasOutput && christmasOutput.textContent) || '');
                christmasCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => christmasCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });

        // Clear button
        christmasClearBtn && christmasClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            selectedChristmasItem = null;
            if (christmasCount) christmasCount.value = '';
            if (christmasPriceCustom) christmasPriceCustom.value = '';
            if (christmasItemsChips) christmasItemsChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            if (christmasFormat) christmasFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            if (christmasActionGroup) {
                [...christmasActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                christmasActionGroup.children[0].classList.add('active');
            }
            christmasUpdate();
        });

        // Initialize
        christmasUpdate();

        // ================= Seeds and fruits Section Formatter =================
        const seedsCategory = document.getElementById('seedsCategory');
        const seedsActionGroup = document.getElementById('seedsActionGroup');
        const fruitsContainer = document.getElementById('fruitsContainer');
        const seedsContainer = document.getElementById('seedsContainer');
        const fruitsChips = document.getElementById('fruitsChips');
        const vegetablesChips = document.getElementById('vegetablesChips');
        const seedsChips = document.getElementById('seedsChips');
        const seedsPriceCustom = document.getElementById('seedsPriceCustom');
        const seedsOutput = document.getElementById('seedsOutput');
        const seedsCopyBtn = document.getElementById('seedsCopyBtn');
        const seedsClearBtn = document.getElementById('seedsClearBtn');

        function getActiveSeeds(container) {
            const a = container && container.querySelector('.active');
            return a ? a.dataset.value : '';
        }

        // mapping which items are vegetables (only cabbage per rules)
        const vegetablesSet = new Set(['cabbage', 'pumpkin']);

        function setActiveSeedsRadio(container, el) {
            [...container.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            seedsUpdate();
        }

        // Toggle category display
        seedsCategory && seedsCategory.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            setActiveSeedsRadio(seedsCategory, el);
            const val = el.dataset.value;
            if (val === 'fruits') {
                fruitsContainer.style.display = '';
                seedsContainer.style.display = 'none';
            } else {
                fruitsContainer.style.display = 'none';
                seedsContainer.style.display = '';
            }
        });

        // Action radios
        seedsActionGroup && seedsActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');

        });

        // Chips click handlers
        fruitsChips && fruitsChips.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            const item = chip.dataset.item;
            // build sentence according to action and whether item is vegetable
            const action = getActiveSeeds(seedsActionGroup) || 'Selling';
            const kind = vegetablesSet.has(item) ? 'vegetables' : 'fruits';
            const priceText = (seedsPriceCustom && seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) ? parseCustomPrice(seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const sentence = `${action} ${item} ${kind}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            if (seedsOutput) seedsOutput.textContent = sentence;
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(sentence);
                chip.classList.add('active');
                setTimeout(() => chip.classList.remove('active'), 300);
            } catch { }
        });

        vegetablesChips && vegetablesChips.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            const item = chip.dataset.item;
            // build sentence according to action and whether item is vegetable
            const action = getActiveSeeds(seedsActionGroup) || 'Selling';
            const kind = 'vegetables';
            const priceText = (seedsPriceCustom && seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) ? parseCustomPrice(seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const sentence = `${action} ${item} ${kind}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            if (seedsOutput) seedsOutput.textContent = sentence;
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(sentence);
                chip.classList.add('active');
                setTimeout(() => chip.classList.remove('active'), 300);
            } catch { }
        });

        seedsChips && seedsChips.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            const item = chip.dataset.item;
            const action = getActiveSeeds(seedsActionGroup) || 'Buying';
            const priceText = (seedsPriceCustom && seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) ? parseCustomPrice(seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            let sentence = '';
            if (item === 'allSeeds') {
                sentence = `${action} seeds. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            } else {
                sentence = `${action} ${item} seeds. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            }
            if (seedsOutput) seedsOutput.textContent = sentence;
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(sentence);
                chip.classList.add('active');
                setTimeout(() => chip.classList.remove('active'), 300);
            } catch { }
        });

        // Update preview when price or actions change
        seedsPriceCustom && seedsPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', seedsUpdate);

        function seedsUpdate() {
            // If a chip is active show its sentence; otherwise show defaults
            const activeFruit = fruitsChips && fruitsChips.querySelector('.chip.active');
            const activeSeed = seedsChips && seedsChips.querySelector('.chip.active');
            const action = getActiveSeeds(seedsActionGroup) || 'Selling';
            const priceText = (seedsPriceCustom && seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) ? parseCustomPrice(seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            if (activeFruit) {
                const item = activeFruit.dataset.item;
                const kind = vegetablesSet.has(item) ? 'vegetables' : 'fruits';
                seedsOutput.textContent = `${action} ${item} ${kind}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                return;
            }
            if (activeSeed) {
                const item = activeSeed.dataset.item;
                if (item === 'allSeeds') seedsOutput.textContent = `${action} seeds. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                else seedsOutput.textContent = `${action} ${item} seeds. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                return;
            }
            // defaults depending on category
            const cat = (seedsCategory && seedsCategory.querySelector('.active') && seedsCategory.querySelector('.active').dataset.value) || 'fruits';
            if (cat === 'fruits') {
                seedsOutput.textContent = `Selling pineapple fruits. Price: Negotiable.`;
            } else {
                seedsOutput.textContent = `Buying seeds. Budget: Negotiable.`;
            }
        }

        seedsCopyBtn && seedsCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((seedsOutput && seedsOutput.textContent || '').trim());
                seedsCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => seedsCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        seedsClearBtn && seedsClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { // reset
            if (seedsPriceCustom) seedsPriceCustom.value = '';
            if (seedsActionGroup) setActiveSeedsRadio(seedsActionGroup, seedsActionGroup.children[0]);
            if (seedsCategory) setActiveSeedsRadio(seedsCategory, seedsCategory.children[0]);
            if (fruitsChips) fruitsChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            if (seedsChips) seedsChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            fruitsContainer.style.display = '';
            seedsContainer.style.display = 'none';
            seedsUpdate();
        });

        // each / in bulk buttons for seeds
        const seedsEachBtn = document.getElementById('seedsEachBtn');
        const seedsBulkBtn = document.getElementById('seedsBulkBtn');

        function seedTypeLabelForCategory() {
            // return currently selected item label and whether it's treated as 'vegetables' or 'fruits' or 'seeds'
            const activeFruit = fruitsChips && fruitsChips.querySelector('.chip.active');
            const activeSeed = seedsChips && seedsChips.querySelector('.chip.active');
            if (activeFruit) {
                const item = activeFruit.dataset.item;
                const kind = vegetablesSet.has(item) ? 'vegetables' : 'fruits';
                return {
                    item,
                    kind,
                    isSeed: false
                };
            }
            if (activeSeed) {
                const item = activeSeed.dataset.item;
                return {
                    item,
                    kind: 'seeds',
                    isSeed: true
                };
            }
            // fallback based on category switch
            const cat = (seedsCategory && seedsCategory.querySelector('.active') && seedsCategory.querySelector('.active').dataset.value) || 'fruits';
            if (cat === 'fruits') return {
                item: 'pineapple',
                kind: 'fruits',
                isSeed: false
            };
            return {
                item: 'seeds',
                kind: 'seeds',
                isSeed: true
            };
        }

        function buildSeedsEachAd() {
            const action = getActiveSeeds(seedsActionGroup) || 'Selling';
            const priceText = (seedsPriceCustom && seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) ? parseCustomPrice(seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const info = seedTypeLabelForCategory();
            // singular/plural: for fruits use singular article when not selected with count
            if (info.isSeed) {
                if (info.item === 'seeds') {
                    return `${action} seeds. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText).replace(/\.$/, '')} each.`;
                }
                return `${action} ${info.item} seeds. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText).replace(/\.$/, '')} each.`;
            }
            // fruits/vegetables
            const label = `${info.item} ${info.kind}`;
            return `${action} ${label}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText).replace(/\.$/, '')} each.`;
        }

        function buildSeedsBulkAd() {
            const action = getActiveSeeds(seedsActionGroup) || 'Selling';
            const priceText = (seedsPriceCustom && seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) ? parseCustomPrice(seedsPriceCustom.value.trim()) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const info = seedTypeLabelForCategory();
            if (info.isSeed) {
                if (info.item === 'seeds') {
                    return `${action} seeds in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                }
                const plural = `${info.item} seeds`;
                return `${action} ${plural} in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            }
            const plural = `${info.item} ${info.kind}`;
            return `${action} ${plural} in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        seedsEachBtn && seedsEachBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (seedsOutput) seedsOutput.textContent = buildSeedsEachAd();
        });
        seedsBulkBtn && seedsBulkBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (seedsOutput) seedsOutput.textContent = buildSeedsBulkAd();
        });

        // initialize
        seedsUpdate();

        // ================= Juices Section Formatter =================
        const juicesActionGroup = document.getElementById('juicesActionGroup');
        const juicesChips = document.getElementById('juicesChips');
        const juicesPriceCustom = document.getElementById('juicesPriceCustom');
        const juicesOutput = document.getElementById('juicesOutput');
        const juicesCopyBtn = document.getElementById('juicesCopyBtn');
        const juicesClearBtn = document.getElementById('juicesClearBtn');
        const juicesEachBtn = document.getElementById('juicesEachBtn');
        const juicesBulkBtn = document.getElementById('juicesBulkBtn');
        const juicesQuantity = document.getElementById('juicesQuantity');
        const juicesPercent = document.getElementById('juicesPercent');

        // Support multi-select juices. Use the global Quantity field (`#juicesQuantity`) as the count
        // for selected items (per-user request the per-chip quantity input was removed).
        function getActiveJuicesList() {
            const nodes = juicesChips ? [...juicesChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active')] : [];
            // read global quantity if provided and numeric
            const qRaw = (juicesQuantity && (juicesQuantity.value || '').toString().trim()) || '';
            const q = (/^\d+$/.test(qRaw) && Number(qRaw) > 0) ? Number(qRaw) : null;
            const pctRaw = (juicesPercent && (juicesPercent.value || '').toString().trim()) || '';
            return nodes.map(n => {
                let label = (n.dataset.label || n.textContent.trim());
                // apply percent selection
                try {
                    const hasPercentInLabel = /\d+%/.test(label);
                    const isPrefixJuice = /^juice\s+/i.test(label);
                    const base = juiceBase(label);
                    if (pctRaw) {
                        if (hasPercentInLabel) {
                            label = label.replace(/\d+%/, pctRaw);
                        } else if (!isPrefixJuice) {
                            // prepend percent before the base (keep 'juice' suffix if present)
                            // if original label ended with 'juice', produce e.g. '20% attack juice'
                            if (/\s+juice$/i.test(label)) {
                                label = `${pctRaw} ${base} juice`;
                            } else {
                                label = `${pctRaw} ${label}`;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (err) { /* defensive - fallback to original label */ }
                return {
                    key: n.dataset.item || '',
                    label,
                    count: q
                };
            });
        }

        // helper to remove leading/trailing 'juice' tokens for base names
        function juiceBase(label) {
            if (!label) return '';
            const s = label.trim();
            if (/^juice\s+/i.test(s)) return s.replace(/^juice\s+/i, '').trim();
            if (/\s+juice$/i.test(s)) return s.replace(/\s+juice$/i, '').trim();
            return s;
        }

        function buildJuicesSubject(parts) {
            // parts: array of {label, count}
            if (parts.length === 0) return 'juices';
            // special allJuices key
            if (parts.length === 1 && parts[0].key === 'allJuices') return 'juices';
            // if multiple parts
            if (parts.length > 1) {
                const hasCounts = parts.some(p => p.count);
                if (hasCounts) {
                    const segs = parts.map(p => {
                        const base = juiceBase(p.label);
                        return (p.count ? `${p.count} ${base}` : base);
                    });
                    return segs.join(' and ') + ' juices';
                }
                // no counts -> join base names and append 'juices'
                const bases = parts.map(p => juiceBase(p.label));
                if (bases.length === 2) return `${bases[0]} and ${bases[1]} juices`;
                const last = bases.pop();
                return `${bases.join(', ')} and ${last} juices`;
            }
            // single part
            const p = parts[0];
            if (p.key === 'allJuices') return 'juices';
            const lb = p.label.trim();
            const base = juiceBase(lb);
            // If original label started with 'juice ' (prefix), produce 'juices <base>' when count present
            if (/^juice\s+/i.test(lb)) {
                if (p.count) return `${p.count} juices ${base}`;
                return `${lb}`; // 'juice on becoming an animal' -> keep
            }
            if (p.count) {
                // count with suffix-style labels: 'fast running' -> '10 fast running juices'
                return `${p.count} ${base} juices`;
            }
            // no count, single: keep original label (e.g., 'fast running juice')
            return lb;
        }

        function buildJuicesEachAd() {
            const action = (juicesActionGroup && juicesActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active') && juicesActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active').dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            const rawPrice = (juicesPriceCustom && juicesPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceOut = rawPrice ? parseCustomPrice(rawPrice) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const parts = getActiveJuicesList();
            // if no selection, but percent chosen -> use percent-only subject
            const pctRaw = (juicesPercent && (juicesPercent.value || '').toString().trim()) || '';

            // Check if we have a quantity but no specific juice type selected
            const qRaw = (juicesQuantity && (juicesQuantity.value || '').toString().trim()) || '';
            const q = (/^\d+$/.test(qRaw) && Number(qRaw) > 0) ? Number(qRaw) : null;

            if (parts.length === 0) {
                // No specific juice types selected
                if (pctRaw && q) {
                    // Both percentage and quantity provided
                    const subject = `${q} ${pctRaw} juices`;
                    if (/^[\$]/.test(priceOut)) return `${action} ${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${priceOut} each.`;
                    return `${action} ${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                } else if (q) {
                    // Only quantity provided
                    const subject = `${q} juices`;
                    if (/^[\$]/.test(priceOut)) return `${action} ${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${priceOut} each.`;
                    return `${action} ${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                } else if (pctRaw) {
                    // Only percentage provided
                    const subject = `${pctRaw} juices`;
                    if (/^[\$]/.test(priceOut)) return `${action} ${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${priceOut} each.`;
                    return `${action} ${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                } else {
                    // Neither quantity nor percentage provided
                    return `${action} juices. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                }
            }
            const subject = buildJuicesSubject(parts.map((p, i) => ({
                key: (juicesChips.children[i] && juicesChips.children[i].dataset.item) || '',
                label: p.label,
                count: p.count
            })));
            // If priceOut is numeric ($...), use 'each.' punctuation for numeric price
            if (/^\$/.test(priceOut)) {
                return `${action} ${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${priceOut} each.`;
            }
            return `${action} ${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
        }

        function buildJuicesBulkAd() {
            const action = (juicesActionGroup && juicesActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active') && juicesActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active').dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            const rawPrice = (juicesPriceCustom && juicesPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceOut = rawPrice ? parseCustomPrice(rawPrice) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const parts = getActiveJuicesList();
            const pctRaw = (juicesPercent && (juicesPercent.value || '').toString().trim()) || '';

            // Check if we have a quantity but no specific juice type selected
            const qRaw = (juicesQuantity && (juicesQuantity.value || '').toString().trim()) || '';
            const q = (/^\d+$/.test(qRaw) && Number(qRaw) > 0) ? Number(qRaw) : null;

            if (parts.length === 0) {
                // No specific juice types selected
                if (pctRaw && q) {
                    // Both percentage and quantity provided
                    return `${action} ${q} ${pctRaw} juices in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                } else if (q) {
                    // Only quantity provided
                    return `${action} ${q} juices in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                } else if (pctRaw) {
                    // Only percentage provided
                    return `${action} ${pctRaw} juices in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                } else {
                    // Neither quantity nor percentage provided
                    return `${action} juices in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                }
            }
            const subject = buildJuicesSubject(parts.map((p, i) => ({
                key: (juicesChips.children[i] && juicesChips.children[i].dataset.item) || '',
                label: p.label,
                count: p.count
            })));
            return `${action} ${subject} in bulk. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
        }

        function juicesUpdate() {
            const parts = getActiveJuicesList();
            const action = (juicesActionGroup && juicesActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active') && juicesActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active').dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            const rawPrice = (juicesPriceCustom && juicesPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceOut = rawPrice ? parseCustomPrice(rawPrice) : 'Negotiable';
            const pctRaw = (juicesPercent && (juicesPercent.value || '').toString().trim()) || '';

            // Check if we have a quantity but no specific juice type selected
            const qRaw = (juicesQuantity && (juicesQuantity.value || '').toString().trim()) || '';
            const q = (/^\d+$/.test(qRaw) && Number(qRaw) > 0) ? Number(qRaw) : null;

            if (parts.length === 0) {
                // No specific juice types selected
                if (pctRaw && q) {
                    // Both percentage and quantity provided
                    juicesOutput.textContent = `${action} ${q} ${pctRaw} juices. ${(action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price')}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                    return;
                } else if (q) {
                    // Only quantity provided
                    juicesOutput.textContent = `${action} ${q} juices. ${(action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price')}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                    return;
                } else if (pctRaw) {
                    // Only percentage provided
                    juicesOutput.textContent = `${action} ${pctRaw} juices. ${(action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price')}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                    return;
                } else {
                    // Neither quantity nor percentage provided
                    juicesOutput.textContent = `${action} juices. ${(action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price')}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
                    return;
                }
            }

            const subject = buildJuicesSubject(parts.map(p => ({
                key: p.key,
                label: p.label,
                count: p.count
            })));
            juicesOutput.textContent = `${action} ${subject}. ${(action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price')}: ${punctuatePrice(priceOut)}`;
        }
        // Clicking a chip toggles its active state. Quantity is taken from the global
        // `#juicesQuantity` input (if provided).
        juicesChips && juicesChips.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            chip.classList.toggle('active');
            juicesUpdate();
        });
        juicesActionGroup && juicesActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            [...juicesActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            juicesUpdate();
        });
        juicesPriceCustom && juicesPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', juicesUpdate);
        juicesQuantity && juicesQuantity.addEventListener('input', juicesUpdate);
        juicesPercent && juicesPercent.addEventListener('change', juicesUpdate);

        // Handle quantity helper input and button
        const juicesQuantityNum = document.getElementById('juicesQuantityNum');
        const juicesQuantityBtn = document.getElementById('juicesQuantityBtn');
        juicesQuantityBtn && juicesQuantityBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const val = juicesQuantityNum && juicesQuantityNum.value;
            if (val && juicesQuantity) {
                juicesQuantity.value = val;
                juicesUpdate();
            }
        });
        juicesQuantityNum && juicesQuantityNum.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                const val = juicesQuantityNum.value;
                if (val && juicesQuantity) {
                    juicesQuantity.value = val;
                    juicesUpdate();
                }
            }
        });
        juicesEachBtn && juicesEachBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (juicesOutput) juicesOutput.textContent = buildJuicesEachAd();
        });
        juicesBulkBtn && juicesBulkBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (juicesOutput) juicesOutput.textContent = buildJuicesBulkAd();
        });
        juicesCopyBtn && juicesCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((juicesOutput && juicesOutput.textContent || '').trim());
                juicesCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => juicesCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        juicesClearBtn && juicesClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (juicesPriceCustom) juicesPriceCustom.value = '';
            if (juicesQuantity) juicesQuantity.value = '';
            if (juicesPercent) juicesPercent.value = '';
            if (juicesActionGroup) setActive(juicesActionGroup, juicesActionGroup.children[0]);
            if (juicesChips) juicesChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            juicesUpdate();
        });
        juicesUpdate();



        // ================= Fish Section Formatter =================
        const fishActionGroup = document.getElementById('fishActionGroup');
        const fishChips = document.getElementById('fishChips');

        function buildFishAd() {
            const action = (fishActionGroup && fishActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active') && fishActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active').dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            const rawPrice = (fishPriceCustom && fishPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
            const priceOut = rawPrice ? parseCustomPrice(rawPrice) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const activeChip = fishChips && fishChips.querySelector('.chip.active');
            const qtyRaw = (fishQuantity && fishQuantity.value || '').trim();
            const qty = /^\d+$/.test(qtyRaw) ? Number(qtyRaw) : null;

            if (!activeChip) {
                return 'Select a fish type to see preview.';
            }

            const fishType = activeChip.dataset.item;

            // Get selected formats
            const formatChips = document.querySelectorAll('#fishFormat .chip.active');
            const formats = Array.from(formatChips).map(chip => chip.dataset.format);
            const isEach = formats.includes('each');
            const isBulk = formats.includes('bulk');

            let text = `${action} `;
            if (qty) {
                text += `${qty} `;
            }
            text += fishType;
            if (isBulk) {
                text += ' in bulk';
            }
            text += '. ';
            text += `${priceWord}: `;
            if (isEach) {
                text += `${priceOut} each.`;
            } else {
                text += punctuatePrice(priceOut);
            }

            return text;
        }

        function fishUpdate() {
            fishOutput.textContent = buildFishAd();
