        // ================= Ticket / Prime Section Formatter =================
        const ticketChipsEl = document.getElementById('ticketChips');
        const ticketActionEl = document.getElementById('ticketActionGroup');
        const ticketCountInput = document.getElementById('ticketCount');
        const ticketIncBtn = document.getElementById('ticketInc');
        const ticketDecBtn = document.getElementById('ticketDec');
        const ticketPriceCustomInput = document.getElementById('ticketPriceCustom');
        const ticketOutputEl = document.getElementById('ticketOutput');
        const ticketCopyBtnEl = document.getElementById('ticketCopyBtn');
        const ticketClearBtnEl = document.getElementById('ticketClearBtn');

        let ticketSelectedType = null;
        let ticketSelectedFormat = null; // Track selected format ('each', 'bulk', or null) // e.g. 'regular', 'rare', ...

        function ticketTypeLabel(type) {
            const map = {
                regular: 'regular lottery ticket',
                rare: 'rare lottery ticket',
                flamewater: 'flame and water lottery ticket',
                cayo: 'Cayo Perico ticket',
                car: 'Car ticket',
                secretFragment: 'Secret ticket fragment',
                secret: 'Secret ticket',
                resourceMiners: 'Resource Miners ticket',
                grand: 'Grand ticket'
            };
            return map[type] || 'VIP Ticket';
        }

        function getTicketAction() {
            const a = ticketActionEl && ticketActionEl.querySelector('.radio.active');
            return a ? a.dataset.value : 'Selling';
        }

        function buildTicketAd() {
            const action = getTicketAction();
            const type = ticketSelectedType || 'regular';
            const label = ticketTypeLabel(type);
            const countRaw = (ticketCountInput && ticketCountInput.value || '').toString().trim();
            const count = countRaw === '' ? undefined : (Number.isFinite(Number(countRaw)) ? Math.max(1, parseInt(countRaw, 10)) : undefined);
            const customRaw = (ticketPriceCustomInput && ticketPriceCustomInput.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

            let subject = '';
            if (count && count > 1) {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${label.endsWith('s') ? label : (label + 's')}`;
            } else {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${aOrAn(label)} ${label}`;
            }

            return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function aOrAn(phrase) {
            return /^[aeiou]/i.test(phrase) ? 'an' : 'a';
        }

        function ticketUpdate() {
            if (!ticketOutputEl) return;

            // Use the appropriate function based on selected format
            if (ticketSelectedFormat === 'each') {
                ticketOutputEl.textContent = buildEachAd();
            } else if (ticketSelectedFormat === 'bulk') {
                ticketOutputEl.textContent = buildBulkAd();
            } else {
                ticketOutputEl.textContent = buildTicketAd();
            }
        }

        // Chips selection
        ticketChipsEl && ticketChipsEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
            if (!chip) return;
            // mark active
            [...ticketChipsEl.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            chip.classList.add('active');
            ticketSelectedType = chip.dataset.type;
            ticketSelectedFormat = null; // Reset format when selecting new ticket type
            ticketUpdate();
            // try copying the template text as well
            try {
                navigator.clipboard.writeText(buildTicketAd());
                chip.classList.add('active');
                setTimeout(() => chip.classList.remove('active'), 300);
            } catch { }
        });

        // Action radios
        ticketActionEl && ticketActionEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            [...ticketActionEl.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            ticketUpdate();
        });

        // Count buttons and input
        ticketIncBtn && ticketIncBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const v = Number(ticketCountInput.value || 0) || 0;
            ticketCountInput.value = Math.max(1, v + 1);
            ticketUpdate();
        });
        ticketDecBtn && ticketDecBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const v = Number(ticketCountInput.value || 0) || 0;
            ticketCountInput.value = Math.max(1, v - 1);
            ticketUpdate();
        });
        ticketCountInput && ticketCountInput.addEventListener('input', ticketUpdate);

        // Price input
        ticketPriceCustomInput && ticketPriceCustomInput.addEventListener('input', ticketUpdate);

        // Copy & Clear
        ticketCopyBtnEl && ticketCopyBtnEl.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((ticketOutputEl && ticketOutputEl.textContent || '').trim());
                ticketCopyBtnEl.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => ticketCopyBtnEl.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        ticketClearBtnEl && ticketClearBtnEl.addEventListener('click', () => {
            ticketSelectedType = null;
            ticketSelectedFormat = null; // Reset format when clearing
            if (ticketChipsEl) [...ticketChipsEl.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            if (ticketCountInput) ticketCountInput.value = '1';
            if (ticketPriceCustomInput) ticketPriceCustomInput.value = '';
            if (ticketActionEl) {
                [...ticketActionEl.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                ticketActionEl.children[0].classList.add('active');
            }
            ticketUpdate();
        });

        ticketUpdate();

        // ================= Prime Section Formatter (mirror of Ticket) =================
        const primeChipsEl = document.getElementById('primeChips');
        const primeActionEl = document.getElementById('primeActionGroup');
        const primeCountInput = document.getElementById('primeCount');
        const primeIncBtn = document.getElementById('primeInc');
        const primeDecBtn = document.getElementById('primeDec');
        const primePriceCustomInput = document.getElementById('primePriceCustom');
        const primeOutputEl = document.getElementById('primeOutput');
        const primeCopyBtnEl = document.getElementById('primeCopyBtn');
        const primeClearBtnEl = document.getElementById('primeClearBtn');

        let primeSelectedType = null;

        function primeTypeLabel(type) {
            const map = {
                primeRegular: 'Prime',
                primeRare: 'Prime rare',
                primeEvent: 'Prime event',
                primeCar: 'Prime Car'
            };
            return map[type] || 'Prime item';
        }

        function getPrimeAction() {
            const a = primeActionEl && primeActionEl.querySelector('.radio.active');
            return a ? a.dataset.value : 'Selling';
        }

        function buildPrimeAd() {
            const action = getPrimeAction();
            const type = primeSelectedType || 'primeRegular';
            const label = primeTypeLabel(type);
            const countRaw = (primeCountInput && primeCountInput.value || '').toString().trim();
            const count = countRaw === '' ? undefined : (Number.isFinite(Number(countRaw)) ? Math.max(1, parseInt(countRaw, 10)) : undefined);
            const customRaw = (primePriceCustomInput && primePriceCustomInput.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

            let subject = '';
            if (count && count > 1) {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${label.endsWith('s') ? label : (label + 's')}`;
            } else {
                subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${aOrAn(label)} ${label}`;
            }

            return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function primeUpdate() {

        }

        primeChipsEl && primeChipsEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {

        });
        primeActionEl && primeActionEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {

        });

        });
        primeDecBtn && primeDecBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {

            primeCountInput.value = Math.max(1, v - 1);
            primeUpdate();
        });
        primeCountInput && primeCountInput.addEventListener('input', primeUpdate);
        primePriceCustomInput && primePriceCustomInput.addEventListener('input', primeUpdate);
        primeCopyBtnEl && primeCopyBtnEl.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((primeOutputEl && primeOutputEl.textContent || '').trim());
                primeCopyBtnEl.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => primeCopyBtnEl.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        primeClearBtnEl && primeClearBtnEl.addEventListener('click', () => {
            primeSelectedType = null;
            if (primeChipsEl) [...primeChipsEl.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            if (primeCountInput) primeCountInput.value = '1';
            if (primePriceCustomInput) primePriceCustomInput.value = '';
            if (primeActionEl) {
                [...primeActionEl.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                primeActionEl.children[0].classList.add('active');
            }
            primeUpdate();
        });

        // each / in bulk for prime
        const primeEachBtn = document.getElementById('primeEachBtn');
        const primeBulkBtn = document.getElementById('primeBulkBtn');

        function buildPrimeEachAd() {
            const action = getPrimeAction();
            const type = primeSelectedType || 'primeRegular';
            const label = primeTypeLabel(type);
            const customRaw = (primePriceCustomInput && primePriceCustomInput.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            const countRaw = (primeCountInput && primeCountInput.value || '').toString().trim();
            const count = countRaw === '' ? undefined : (Number.isFinite(Number(countRaw)) ? Math.max(1, parseInt(countRaw, 10)) : undefined);
            const subject = (count && count > 1) ? `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${label.endsWith('s') ? label : (label + 's')}` : `${capitalize(action)} ${aOrAn(label)} ${label}`;
            return `${subject}. Price: ${punctuatePrice(priceText).replace(/\.$/, '')} each.`;
        }

        function buildPrimeBulkAd() {

            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            const plural = label.endsWith('s') ? label : (label + 's');
            const subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${plural} in bulk`;
            return `${subject}. Price: ${punctuatePrice(priceText).replace(/\.$/, '')} each.`;

        // each / in bulk buttons
        const ticketEachBtn = document.getElementById('ticketEachBtn');

        function buildEachAd() {
            const action = getTicketAction();
            const type = ticketSelectedType || 'regular';
            const label = ticketTypeLabel(type);
            const customRaw = (ticketPriceCustomInput && ticketPriceCustomInput.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            // "Selling 7 Grand tickets. Price: $100.000 each." or
            // "Buying a Grand ticket. Budget: $100.000 each."
            const countRaw = (ticketCountInput && ticketCountInput.value || '').toString().trim();
            const count = countRaw === '' ? undefined : (Number.isFinite(Number(countRaw)) ? Math.max(1, parseInt(countRaw, 10)) : undefined);
            const subject = (count && count > 1) ? `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${label.endsWith('s') ? label : (label + 's')}` : `${capitalize(action)} ${aOrAn(label)} ${label}`;
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText).replace(/\.$/, '')} each.`;
        }

        function buildBulkAd() {
            const action = getTicketAction();
            const type = ticketSelectedType || 'regular';
            const label = ticketTypeLabel(type);
            const customRaw = (ticketPriceCustomInput && ticketPriceCustomInput.value || '').trim();
            const priceText = customRaw ? parseCustomPrice(customRaw) : 'Negotiable';
            // "Selling Grand tickets in bulk. Price: $100.000 each."
            const plural = label.endsWith('s') ? label : (label + 's');
            const subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${plural} in bulk`;
            const priceWord = (action === 'Buying') ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

            // Check if price is numeric to determine if we should add "each"
            if (/^\$/.test(priceText)) {
                return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${priceText} each.`;
            } else {
                return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
            }
        }

        ticketEachBtn && ticketEachBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            ticketSelectedFormat = 'each';

        ticketBulkBtn && ticketBulkBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            ticketSelectedFormat = 'bulk';
            if (ticketOutputEl) ticketOutputEl.textContent = buildBulkAd();
        });

        // Prime Section Handler
        const primeType = document.getElementById('primeType');
        const primeAction = document.getElementById('primeAction');
        const primeDays = document.getElementById('primeDays');
        const primeOptions = document.getElementById('primeOptions');
        const primePrice = document.getElementById('primePrice');
        const primeOutput = document.getElementById('primeOutput');

        function formatPrimeMoney(text) {
            const s = (text || '').trim();
            if (!s) return 'Negotiable';

            // 1) Dotted thousands like 100.000 or $250.000 -> keep format and ensure $ prefix
            if (/^\$?\d{1,3}(?:\.\d{3})+$/.test(s)) {
                return s.startsWith('$') ? s : `$${s}`;
            }

            // 2) Already specified with $ or Million -> normalize Million case
            if (/\$|million/i.test(s)) {
                return s.replace(/million/ig, 'Million');
            }

            // 3) Decimals with ONE or TWO digits after dot -> Million (e.g., 12.5 => $12.5 Million)
            if (/^\d+(?:\.\d{1,2})$/.test(s)) {
                return `$${s} Million`;
            }

            // 4) Pure numeric handling
            const num = Number(s.replace(/[\,\s]/g, ''));
            if (!Number.isNaN(num)) {
                if (num >= 100) {
                    // Convert to dotted thousands (e.g., 250000 -> $250.000)
                    return `$${String(Math.trunc(num)).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.')}`;
                }
                if (num >= 1) {
                    // 1..99 -> Millions
                    return `$${String(Math.trunc(num))} Million`;
                }
            }
            return s;
        }

        function getPrimePrice() {
            const customPrice = document.getElementById('primePriceCustom');
            const selectPrice = document.getElementById('primePriceSelect');

            const custom = (customPrice && customPrice.value || '').trim();
            if (custom) {
                return formatPrimeMoney(custom);
            }
            if (selectPrice && selectPrice.value) {
                return selectPrice.value;
            }
            return 'Negotiable';
        }

        function updatePrimeAd() {
            if (!primeOutput) return;

            const type = primeType?.querySelector('.active')?.getAttribute('data-value') || 'Prime';
            const action = primeAction?.querySelector('.active')?.getAttribute('data-value') || 'Selling';
            const days = primeDays?.value;
            const option = primeOptions?.querySelector('.active')?.getAttribute('data-value');
            const count = document.getElementById('primeCount')?.value;
            const price = getPrimePrice();

            // Remove 'a' or 'regular' from the ad text
            let adText = '';

            // Add count if specified and greater than 1
            if (count && parseInt(count) > 1) {
                adText = `${action} ${count} ${type}`;
            } else {
                adText = `${action} ${type}`;
            }

            if (days) {
                adText += ` with ${days} days`;
            }

            if (option === 'In Bulk') {
                adText += ' in bulk';
            }

            // Build price line using existing punctuatePrice logic so trailing punctuation
            // is correct for both dotted-thousands and Million styles.
            const priceLabel = (action === 'Selling') ? 'Price' : 'Budget';
            const priceLine = `${priceLabel}: ${punctuatePrice(price)}`;

            // Remove any double spaces and ensure proper formatting for subject
            adText = adText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
            // Always use punctuatePrice result as-is for the price part. That function
            // adds a trailing period for 'Million' and 'Negotiable' but not for
            // dotted-thousands like "$100.000". This preserves the desired format.
            primeOutput.textContent = `${adText}. ${priceLine}`.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/\s\./g, '.');
        }

        // Add event listeners for Prime section
        document.querySelectorAll('#primeType .radio, #primeAction .radio, #primeOptions .radio').forEach(radio => {
            radio.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const group = e.target.closest('.radio-group');
                if (!group) return;

                group.querySelectorAll('.radio').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('active'));
                e.target.classList.add('active');
                updatePrimeAd();
            });
        });

        primeDays?.addEventListener('change', updatePrimeAd);
        document.getElementById('primeCount')?.addEventListener('input', updatePrimeAd);
        document.getElementById('primePriceSelect')?.addEventListener('change', updatePrimeAd);
        document.getElementById('primePriceCustom')?.addEventListener('input', updatePrimeAd);

        // Shoulder pets section handling
        (function () {
            const shoulderPetsSection = document.getElementById('shoulderPetsSection');
            if (!shoulderPetsSection) return;

            const shoulderPetsOutput = document.getElementById('shoulderPetsOutput');
            const shoulderPetsCopyBtn = document.getElementById('shoulderPetsCopyBtn');

                    return parseCustomPrice(customRaw);

                if (shoulderPetsPriceSelect && shoulderPetsPriceSelect.value) {

                    shoulderPetsChips.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));

                    selectedPet = e.target.dataset.pet;
                    updatePreview();

                shoulderPetsPriceSelect.addEventListener('change', updatePreview);

            if (shoulderPetsPriceCustom) {
                shoulderPetsPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', updatePreview);

        })(); // Mining Resources section handling
        (function () {
            const miningResourcesSection = document.getElementById('miningResourcesSection');
            if (!miningResourcesSection) return;

            const miningResourcesOutput = document.getElementById('miningResourcesOutput');
            const miningResourcesCopyBtn = document.getElementById('miningResourcesCopyBtn');
            const miningResourcesClearBtn = document.getElementById('miningResourcesClearBtn');
            const miningResourcesActionGroup = document.getElementById('miningResourcesActionGroup');
            const miningResourcesChips = document.getElementById('miningResourcesChips');
            const miningResourcesPriceCustom = document.getElementById('miningResourcesPriceCustom');
            const miningResourcesCount = document.getElementById('miningResourcesCount');
            const miningResourcesFormat = document.getElementById('miningResourcesFormat');

            function capitalize(s) {
                return s ? s[0].toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : s;
            }

            function startsWithVowel(s) {
                return /^[aeiou]/i.test(s);
            }

            function pluralize(s) {
                if (s === 'copper') return 'copper'; // copper is the same in singular and plural
                if (s === 'emerald') return 'emeralds';
                if (s === 'ruby') return 'rubies';
                if (s === 'diamond') return 'diamonds';
                return s + 's';
            }

            function buildMiningResourcesAd() {
                const action = miningResourcesActionGroup && miningResourcesActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
                const activeChip = miningResourcesChips && miningResourcesChips.querySelector('.chip.active');
                if (!activeChip) {
                    return 'Select a resource to generate your ad.';
                }

                const item = activeChip.dataset.item;
                const countRaw = (miningResourcesCount && miningResourcesCount.value || '').trim();
                const count = countRaw && /^\d+$/.test(countRaw) ? parseInt(countRaw, 10) : undefined;
                const rawPrice = (miningResourcesPriceCustom && miningResourcesPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
                const priceText = rawPrice ? parseCustomPrice(rawPrice) : 'Negotiable';
                const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

                // Get selected formats
                const formatChips = miningResourcesFormat && miningResourcesFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip.active') || [];
                const formats = Array.from(formatChips).map(chip => chip.dataset.format);
                const isEach = formats.includes('each');
                const isBulk = formats.includes('bulk');

                let subject = '';

                // Special handling for bulk format - no articles and use plural form directly
                if (isBulk) {
                    if (count && count > 1) {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${pluralize(item)}`;
                    } else {
                        // For bulk with single item, use plural form without article
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${pluralize(item)}`;
                    }
                    subject += ' in bulk';
                } else {
                    // Normal handling (each format or no format)
                    if (count && count > 1) {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${pluralize(item)}`;
                    } else {
                        const article = startsWithVowel(item) ? 'an' : 'a';
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${article} ${item}`;
                    }
                }

                // Handle pricing format
                if (isEach && priceText !== 'Negotiable') {
                    return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${priceText} each.`;
                } else {
                    return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                }
            }

            function miningResourcesUpdate() {
                if (miningResourcesOutput) miningResourcesOutput.textContent = buildMiningResourcesAd();
                // Update price label
                const action = miningResourcesActionGroup && miningResourcesActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
                const label = document.getElementById('miningResourcesPriceLabel');
                if (label) label.textContent = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            }

            miningResourcesActionGroup && miningResourcesActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
                if (!el) return;
                [...miningResourcesActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                el.classList.add('active');
                miningResourcesUpdate();
            });

            miningResourcesChips && miningResourcesChips.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
                const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
                if (!chip) return;
                [...miningResourcesChips.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                chip.classList.add('active');
                miningResourcesUpdate();
            });

            [miningResourcesCount, miningResourcesPriceCustom].forEach(el => el && el.addEventListener('input', miningResourcesUpdate));

            miningResourcesFormat && miningResourcesFormat.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
                if (!chip) return;
                chip.classList.toggle('active');
                miningResourcesUpdate();
            });

            miningResourcesCopyBtn && miningResourcesCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
                try {
                    await navigator.clipboard.writeText((miningResourcesOutput && miningResourcesOutput.textContent) || '');
                    miningResourcesCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                    setTimeout(() => miningResourcesCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
                } catch {
                    alert('Copy failed');
                }
            });

            miningResourcesClearBtn && miningResourcesClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                if (miningResourcesCount) miningResourcesCount.value = '';
                if (miningResourcesPriceCustom) miningResourcesPriceCustom.value = '';
                if (miningResourcesChips) miningResourcesChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                if (miningResourcesFormat) miningResourcesFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                if (miningResourcesActionGroup) {
                    [...miningResourcesActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    miningResourcesActionGroup.children[0].classList.add('active');
                }
                miningResourcesUpdate();
            });

            // Initialize
            miningResourcesUpdate();
        })();

        // ================= ACCESSORY Section Formatter =================
        const accessoryActionGroup = document.getElementById('accessoryActionGroup');
        const accessoryGenderGroup = document.getElementById('accessoryGenderGroup');
        const accessoryItem = document.getElementById('accessoryItem');
        const accessoryItemType1 = document.getElementById('accessoryItemType1');
        const accessoryItemType2 = document.getElementById('accessoryItemType2');
        const accessoryPriceCustom = document.getElementById('accessoryPriceCustom');
        const accessoryPriceLabel = document.getElementById('accessoryPriceLabel');
        const accessoryOutput = document.getElementById('accessoryOutput');
        const accessoryCopyBtn = document.getElementById('accessoryCopyBtn');
        const accessoryClearBtn = document.getElementById('accessoryClearBtn');

        function buildAccessoryText() {
            const action = (accessoryActionGroup && accessoryActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || 'Selling';
            const gender = (accessoryGenderGroup && accessoryGenderGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value) || '';
            const item = (accessoryItem && accessoryItem.value.trim()) || 'accessory';
            const itemType1 = (accessoryItemType1 && accessoryItemType1.value.trim()) || '';
            const itemType2 = (accessoryItemType2 && accessoryItemType2.value.trim()) || '';
            const label = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            const priceRaw = accessoryPriceCustom ? accessoryPriceCustom.value.trim() : '';
            const priceText = priceRaw ? parseCustomPrice(priceRaw) : 'Negotiable';

            // Build item description
            let itemDesc = item;

            // Apply "of type" format if item types are provided
            if (itemType1 && itemType2) {
                itemDesc = `${item} of type ${itemType1} and ${itemType2}`;
            } else if (itemType1) {
                itemDesc = `${item} of type ${itemType1}`;
            }

            // Add gender specification only if selected
            const genderText = gender ? ` for ${gender.toLowerCase()}` : '';

            return `${capitalize(action)} ${itemDesc}${genderText}. ${label}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
        }

        function accessoryUpdate() {
            if (accessoryOutput) accessoryOutput.textContent = buildAccessoryText();
        }

        accessoryActionGroup && accessoryActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            [...accessoryActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            if (accessoryPriceLabel) accessoryPriceLabel.textContent = el.dataset.value === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            accessoryUpdate();
        });

        accessoryGenderGroup && accessoryGenderGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
            if (!el) return;
            [...accessoryGenderGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            accessoryUpdate();
        });

        accessoryItem && accessoryItem.addEventListener('input', accessoryUpdate);
        accessoryItem && accessoryItem.addEventListener('change', accessoryUpdate);
        accessoryItemType1 && accessoryItemType1.addEventListener('input', accessoryUpdate);
        accessoryItemType1 && accessoryItemType1.addEventListener('change', accessoryUpdate);
        accessoryItemType2 && accessoryItemType2.addEventListener('input', accessoryUpdate);
        accessoryItemType2 && accessoryItemType2.addEventListener('change', accessoryUpdate);
        accessoryPriceCustom && accessoryPriceCustom.addEventListener('input', accessoryUpdate);

        accessoryCopyBtn && accessoryCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText((accessoryOutput && accessoryOutput.textContent) || '');
                accessoryCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => accessoryCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
            } catch {
                alert('Copy failed');
            }
        });
        accessoryClearBtn && accessoryClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (accessoryItem) accessoryItem.value = '';
            if (accessoryItemType1) accessoryItemType1.value = '';
            if (accessoryItemType2) accessoryItemType2.value = '';
            if (accessoryPriceCustom) accessoryPriceCustom.value = '';
            if (accessoryActionGroup) {
                [...accessoryActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                accessoryActionGroup.children[0].classList.add('active');
            }
            if (accessoryGenderGroup) {
                [...accessoryGenderGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            }
            if (accessoryPriceLabel) accessoryPriceLabel.textContent = 'Price';
            accessoryUpdate();
        });

        accessoryUpdate();

        // SIM Card & License Plate section handling
        (function () {
            const simLicensePlateSection = document.getElementById('simLicensePlateSection');
            if (!simLicensePlateSection) return;

            const simLicensePlateOutput = document.getElementById('simLicensePlateOutput');
            const simLicensePlateCopyBtn = document.getElementById('simLicensePlateCopyBtn');
            const simLicensePlateClearBtn = document.getElementById('simLicensePlateClearBtn');
            const simLicensePlateActionGroup = document.getElementById('simLicensePlateActionGroup');
            const simLicensePlateItemType = document.getElementById('simLicensePlateItemType');
            const licensePlateCountField = document.getElementById('licensePlateCountField');
            const simCardNumberField = document.getElementById('simCardNumberField');
            const licensePlateInputField = document.getElementById('licensePlateInputField');
            const licensePlateCount = document.getElementById('licensePlateCount');
            const simCardNumber = document.getElementById('simCardNumber');
            const licensePlateValue = document.getElementById('licensePlateValue');
            const simLicensePlatePriceCustom = document.getElementById('simLicensePlatePriceCustom');
            const simLicensePlatePriceLabel = document.getElementById('simLicensePlatePriceLabel');

            function capitalize(s) {
                return s ? s[0].toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : s;
            }

            function buildSimLicensePlateAd() {
                const action = simLicensePlateActionGroup && simLicensePlateActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
                const itemType = simLicensePlateItemType && simLicensePlateItemType.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'licensePlate';
                const priceText = (simLicensePlatePriceCustom && simLicensePlatePriceCustom.value.trim()) ? parseCustomPrice(simLicensePlatePriceCustom.value.trim()) : 'Negotiable';
                const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

                if (itemType === 'licensePlate') {
                    const plateValue = (licensePlateValue && licensePlateValue.value.trim()) || '';
                    const countRaw = (licensePlateCount && licensePlateCount.value) || '';
                    const count = countRaw && /^\d+$/.test(countRaw) ? parseInt(countRaw, 10) : undefined;

                    // Handle different license plate ad formats
                    if (plateValue) {
                        // Specific license plate
                        return `${capitalize(action)} license plate (${plateValue}). ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                    } else if (count && count > 1) {
                        // Multiple license plates
                        return `${capitalize(action)} ${count} license plates. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                    } else if (count === 1) {
                        // Single license plate
                        return `${capitalize(action)} a license plate. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                    } else {
                        // Generic license plate
                        return `${capitalize(action)} a license plate. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                    }
                } else {
                    // SIM Card
                    const cardNumber = (simCardNumber && simCardNumber.value.trim()) || '';

                    if (cardNumber) {
                        // Specific SIM card with number
                        return `${capitalize(action)} SIM card № ${cardNumber}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                    } else {
                        // Generic SIM card
                        return `${capitalize(action)} SIM cards. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                    }
                }
            }

            function updateSimLicensePlatePreview() {
                if (simLicensePlateOutput) simLicensePlateOutput.textContent = buildSimLicensePlateAd();
            }

            function updateItemTypeUI() {
                const itemType = simLicensePlateItemType && simLicensePlateItemType.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'licensePlate';

                if (itemType === 'licensePlate') {
                    if (licensePlateCountField) licensePlateCountField.style.display = '';
                    if (simCardNumberField) simCardNumberField.style.display = 'none';
                    if (licensePlateInputField) licensePlateInputField.style.display = '';
                } else {
                    if (licensePlateCountField) licensePlateCountField.style.display = 'none';
                    if (simCardNumberField) simCardNumberField.style.display = '';
                    if (licensePlateInputField) licensePlateInputField.style.display = 'none';
                }

                updateSimLicensePlatePreview();
            }

            // Event listeners
            simLicensePlateActionGroup && simLicensePlateActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
                if (!el) return;
                [...simLicensePlateActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                el.classList.add('active');

                // Update price label
                const action = el.dataset.value || 'Selling';
                if (simLicensePlatePriceLabel) {
                    simLicensePlatePriceLabel.textContent = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
                }

                updateSimLicensePlatePreview();
            });

            simLicensePlateItemType && simLicensePlateItemType.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
                if (!el) return;
                [...simLicensePlateItemType.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                el.classList.add('active');
                updateItemTypeUI();
            });

            // Input event listeners
            [licensePlateCount, simCardNumber, licensePlateValue, simLicensePlatePriceCustom].forEach(el => {
                el && el.addEventListener('input', updateSimLicensePlatePreview);
            });

            simLicensePlateCopyBtn && simLicensePlateCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
                try {
                    await navigator.clipboard.writeText((simLicensePlateOutput && simLicensePlateOutput.textContent) || '');
                    simLicensePlateCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                    setTimeout(() => simLicensePlateCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
                } catch {
                    alert('Copy failed');
                }
            });

            simLicensePlateClearBtn && simLicensePlateClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                if (licensePlateCount) licensePlateCount.value = '';
                if (simCardNumber) simCardNumber.value = '';
                if (licensePlateValue) licensePlateValue.value = '';
                if (simLicensePlatePriceCustom) simLicensePlatePriceCustom.value = '';

                if (simLicensePlateActionGroup) {
                    [...simLicensePlateActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    simLicensePlateActionGroup.children[0].classList.add('active');
                }

                if (simLicensePlateItemType) {
                    [...simLicensePlateItemType.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    simLicensePlateItemType.children[0].classList.add('active');
                }

                if (simLicensePlatePriceLabel) simLicensePlatePriceLabel.textContent = 'Price';

                updateItemTypeUI();
            });

            // Initialize
            updateItemTypeUI();
            if (simLicensePlatePriceLabel) simLicensePlatePriceLabel.textContent = 'Price';
        })();

        // Resource Barrel section handling
        (function () {
            const resourceBarrelSection = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelSection');
            if (!resourceBarrelSection) return;

            const resourceBarrelOutput = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelOutput');
            const resourceBarrelCopyBtn = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelCopyBtn');
            const resourceBarrelClearBtn = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelClearBtn');
            const resourceBarrelActionGroup = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelActionGroup');
            const resourceBarrelChips = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelChips');
            const resourceBarrelPriceCustom = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelPriceCustom');
            const resourceBarrelCount = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelCount');
            const resourceBarrelFormat = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelFormat');

            // Special non-pluralizable items for resource barrels
            const nonPluralItems = ['solar', 'gasoline', 'kerosene'];

            function capitalize(s) {
                return s ? s[0].toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : s;
            }

            function startsWithVowel(s) {
                return /^[aeiou]/i.test(s);
            }

            function pluralize(s) {
                // These items should not be pluralized
                if (nonPluralItems.includes(s)) return s;
                // Standard pluralization rules
                if (s === 'gasoline') return 'gasolines'; // Though we won't use this due to nonPluralItems
                return s + 's';
            }

            function buildResourceBarrelAd() {
                const action = resourceBarrelActionGroup && resourceBarrelActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
                const activeChip = resourceBarrelChips && resourceBarrelChips.querySelector('.chip.active');
                if (!activeChip) {
                    return 'Select a barrel type to generate your ad.';
                }

                const item = activeChip.dataset.item;
                const countRaw = (resourceBarrelCount && resourceBarrelCount.value || '').trim();
                const count = countRaw && /^\d+$/.test(countRaw) ? parseInt(countRaw, 10) : undefined;
                const rawPrice = (resourceBarrelPriceCustom && resourceBarrelPriceCustom.value || '').trim();
                const priceText = rawPrice ? parseCustomPrice(rawPrice) : 'Negotiable';
                const priceWord = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';

                // Get selected formats
                const formatChips = resourceBarrelFormat && resourceBarrelFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip.active') || [];
                const formats = Array.from(formatChips).map(chip => chip.dataset.format);
                const isEach = formats.includes('each');
                const isBulk = formats.includes('bulk');

                let subject = '';

                // Special handling for bulk format - no articles and use plural form directly
                if (isBulk) {
                    if (count && count > 1) {
                        // For resource barrels, we keep the item name the same even with quantities
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item} barrels`;
                    } else {
                        // For bulk with single item
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${item} barrel`;
                    }
                    subject += ' in bulk';
                } else {
                    // Normal handling (each format or no format)
                    if (count && count > 1) {
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${count} ${item} barrels`;
                    } else {
                        const article = startsWithVowel(item) ? 'an' : 'a';
                        subject = `${capitalize(action)} ${article} ${item} barrel`;
                    }
                }

                // Handle pricing format
                if (isEach && priceText !== 'Negotiable') {
                    return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${priceText} each.`;
                } else {
                    return `${subject}. ${priceWord}: ${punctuatePrice(priceText)}`;
                }
            }

            function resourceBarrelUpdate() {
                if (resourceBarrelOutput) resourceBarrelOutput.textContent = buildResourceBarrelAd();
                // Update price label
                const action = resourceBarrelActionGroup && resourceBarrelActionGroup.querySelector('.radio.active')?.dataset.value || 'Selling';
                const label = document.getElementById('resourceBarrelPriceLabel');
                if (label) label.textContent = action === 'Buying' ? 'Budget' : 'Price';
            }

            resourceBarrelActionGroup && resourceBarrelActionGroup.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const el = e.target.closest('.radio');
                if (!el) return;
                [...resourceBarrelActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                el.classList.add('active');
                resourceBarrelUpdate();
            });

            resourceBarrelChips && resourceBarrelChips.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
                const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
                if (!chip) return;
                [...resourceBarrelChips.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                chip.classList.add('active');
                resourceBarrelUpdate();
            });

            [resourceBarrelCount, resourceBarrelPriceCustom].forEach(el => el && el.addEventListener('input', resourceBarrelUpdate));

            resourceBarrelFormat && resourceBarrelFormat.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const chip = e.target.closest('.chip');
                if (!chip) return;
                chip.classList.toggle('active');
                resourceBarrelUpdate();
            });

            resourceBarrelCopyBtn && resourceBarrelCopyBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
                try {
                    await navigator.clipboard.writeText((resourceBarrelOutput && resourceBarrelOutput.textContent) || '');
                    resourceBarrelCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copied!';
                    setTimeout(() => resourceBarrelCopyBtn.textContent = 'Copy', 1200);
                } catch {
                    alert('Copy failed');
                }
            });

            resourceBarrelClearBtn && resourceBarrelClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                if (resourceBarrelCount) resourceBarrelCount.value = '';
                if (resourceBarrelPriceCustom) resourceBarrelPriceCustom.value = '';
                if (resourceBarrelChips) resourceBarrelChips.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                if (resourceBarrelFormat) resourceBarrelFormat.querySelectorAll('.chip.active').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                if (resourceBarrelActionGroup) {
                    [...resourceBarrelActionGroup.children].forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    resourceBarrelActionGroup.children[0].classList.add('active');
                }
                resourceBarrelUpdate();
            });

            // Initialize
            resourceBarrelUpdate();
        })();
    </script>

    <!-- Password Protection JavaScript -->
    <script>
        // Policy buttons functionality
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            const policyBtn1 = document.getElementById('policyBtn1');
            const policyBtn2 = document.getElementById('policyBtn2');
            const reportButton = document.getElementById('reportButton');
            const releaseNotesBtn = document.getElementById('releaseNotesBtn');
            const logoutBtn = document.getElementById('logoutBtn');

            // Logout button functionality
            if (logoutBtn) {
                logoutBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if (confirm('Are you sure you want to logout?')) {
                        signOut();
                    }
                });
            }

            if (policyBtn1) {
                policyBtn1.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    // Open first policy link in a new tab
                    window.open('https://docs.google.com/document/d/100OFh0lRlShQbYKvMwuYmwj09LSiP25u9NGtFDvo66U/edit?pli=1&tab=t.0', '_blank');
                });
            }

            if (policyBtn2) {
                policyBtn2.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    // Open second policy link in a new tab
                    window.open('https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1ZZwSYM5tJ9sZ9zYoSTVBgJJIr9zvxKRNOkB48A326bU/edit?gid=0#gid=0', '_blank');
                });
            }

            if (reportButton) {
                reportButton.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    // Open Discord link in a new tab
                    window.open('https://discord.gg/f4chfDgjz3', '_blank');
                });
            }

            if (releaseNotesBtn) {
                releaseNotesBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    showSection('updatesSection');
                });
            }
        });
    </script>

    <script>
        // Rejection Reasons Section Functionality
        (function () {
            const rejectionBtn = document.getElementById('rejectionReasonsBtn');
            const rejectionSection = document.getElementById('rejectionReasonsSection');
            const rejectionReasons = document.querySelectorAll('.rejection-reason-item');

            if (!rejectionBtn) return;

            // Show section
            rejectionBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                showSection('rejectionReasonsSection');
            });

            // Handle reason clicks
            rejectionReasons.forEach(reason => {
                reason.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    const text = this.getAttribute('data-reason');
                    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {
                        // Visual feedback
                        const originalText = this.textContent;
                        this.textContent = 'Copied!';
                        this.style.backgroundColor = '#10b981';
                        this.style.color = 'white';
                        this.style.borderColor = '#10b981';

                        setTimeout(() => {
                            this.textContent = originalText;
                            this.style.backgroundColor = '';
                            this.style.color = '';
                            this.style.borderColor = '';
                        }, 1500);
                    }).catch(err => {
                        console.error('Failed to copy: ', err);
                    });
                });
            });
        })();
    </script>
</body>

</html>
